iṉi cūttirattiṉ poruḷ kūṟuvāṉ, “oru cūttirattil ōr uttēciyamum oruvitēyamuntāṉ iruttalvēṇṭum. im muṟaiyai āciriyar tannūluḷ eṅkum āṇṭaṉar eṉpatu an nōkkattuṭaṉ ovvoru cūttirattaiyum ārāyvōrukku naṉku pulappaṭum. iccūttirattil (‘uyartiṇai yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē’ eṉṟu toṭaṅkum iccūttirattil) makkaḷaic cuṭṭuvatu uyartiṇai, makkaḷallāp piṟavaṟṟaic cuṭṭuvatu aḵṟiṇai, avvirutiṇaiyiṉ kaṇṇum col icaikkum eṉṟu mūṉṟu uttēciyamum mūṉṟu vitēyamum iruppatāl vākkiyapētam ākātōveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; itaṉuḷ uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai ivaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkūṟātu muṉṉōr kūṟiya ilakkaṇattaiyē anuvatittu, col eṉpatu uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyiṉkaṇ icaikkum eṉpatu vitikkappaṭukiṉṟateṉka,” eṉa mēlē kaṭāviṭaikaḷāl akalaṅ kūṟakkaruti, vākkiya pētamiṉṟik kūṟum karuttiṉarāy,“ col, makkaḷaic cuṭṭum uyartiṇai, makkaḷal lātataic cuṭṭum aḵṟiṇai ivviraṇṭu tiṇaiyiṉ kaṇṇum icaikkum eṉṟavāṟu” eṉṟu kūṟi muṭittār.
ipporuḷai nōkkumuṉ, “aḵṟiṇai ivviraṇṭu” eṉpataṉai, “aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṟa avviraṇṭu” eṉat tiruttik koḷka, coṟṟoṭar poruṇilai iyaipuṟa niṟṟalvēṇṭu mākaliṉ iccūttirattuḷ vitikkappaṭuvatu, col uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṟa irutiṇaiyiṉ kaṇṇum icaikkumeṉpa tākaliṉ, cūttirapporuḷum ataṉaiyē muṭittaliṉ, vākkiya pētam eytāmai naṉṟāyiṟṟu; āyiṉum, makkaṭcuṭṭu uyar tiṇaiyeṉmaṉār eṉṟiyaiya niṉṟatoṭar, makkaḷaiccuṭṭum uyartiṇai eṉapporuṭantu niṟṟaṟkuriya iyaipiṉmaiyāṉum makkaṭ cuṭṭeṉpatu paṇput tokaip puṟattup piṟanta aṉ moḻit tokaiyeṉṟu cēṉāvaraiyar kūṟiyataṉai uṭam paṭṭup pōntavar, ataṟkuriya poruḷ kūṟātu peyareccamākkik kūṟutal vaḻuvāmākalāṉum, aṉmoḻit tokaiyaip peyareccap poruṇmaittākkutal ilakkaṇamaṟiyār ceyalākalāṉum, eṉmaṉār eṉṉum coṉmuṭipiṉai urai yāciriyarkaḷiṉ uraivikaṟpam kāṭṭit tam karuttiṉaik kāṭṭātu viṭuttatu pōla, īṇṭum eṉmaṉār eṉpataṉai yaṟavē viṭut tamaiyāṉum, ipporuḷiṉkaṇ, vākkiyapētameṉṉum akkuṟṟamoḻiyap piṟakuṟṟam maliyak kiṭattalāṉum ivvurai pōliyurai eṉka. inneṟiyiṉaiyuṇarntaṉṟē, āciriyar cēṉāvaraiyar, “vākkiya pētam eytumāṟu, makkaḷeṉṟu karutappaṭum poruḷai āciriyar uyartiṇai eṉṟu colluvar; makkaḷeṉṟu karutappaṭāta piṟaporuḷai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṟu colluvar; avvirutiṇai mēlum coṟkaḷ nikaḻum eṉṟavāṟu,”  eṉṟu kūṟi, eṉiṉum, itaṉkaṭ kūṟappeṟum ilakkaṇam oṉṟē yeṉa yāppuṟuppār, “eṉavē, uyartiṇai collum aḵṟiṇaic collumeṉac col iraṇṭeṉṟavāṟām,” eṉṟār.
ivar karutiya vākkiyapētam kuṟṟamāy muṭintamaiyiṉ, atupaṟṟi maṟukkappaṭṭaṉavellām kuṟṟamuṭaiya vallaveṉṟu koḷka.
iṉi, innūluḷ kūṟappeṟum uraiyāciriyar iḷampūraṇar eṉpatu tuṇipāyiṉ (uraiyāciriyar vēṟu, iḷampūraṇar vēṟu eṉpārumuḷar) avaruraiyē ellāruraiyiṉum paḻamaiyāṉa teṉpavākaliṉālum, teyvac cilaiyār urai uraiyāciriyar uraikkuk kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭateṉa naṉku teritalālum, avvuraiyāciriyarāl, “ceymmaṉa eṉpatu vaḻakkaritu” eṉak kūṟappaṭum col teyvaccilaiyārkkut tōṉṟutal aritā kalālum, icaikkumaṉ eṉpataṟkuc cēṉāvaraiyar kūṟiyataṉaik kōṭalē poruntuvatām eṉṟātal,piṟitātal kūṟātu kuṟip puraiyāḷar vāḷātu celkiṉṟār.
iṉi, urai nūlkaḷaiyārāyat toṭaṅki ivar, tiru. kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa tolkāppiyac collati kāra iḷampūraṇattu, “eṉmaṉār āciriyar eṉṟu muṭiyaṟpāṟṟu. muṟṟuc col eccappeyar eṉpavākaliṉ, itu ceyyuṇōkkit tokuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu” eṉṟiruttalil, muṭiyaṟpāṟṟu eṉpuḻiyuḷḷa muṟṟuppuḷḷi, kāṟpuḷḷi ( ,)yākavum, eṉpavākaliṉ eṉpuḻiyuḷḷa puḷḷiyai muṟṟup puḷḷiyākavum amaiyat tiruttik koḷḷal vēṇṭumeṉpār, “muṟṟuccol eccappeyar eṉpavākaliṉ eṉpatu eṉmaṉār āciriyar eṉṟu muṭiyaṟpāṟṟu eṉpataṟku ētuvākum” eṉṟār. mēṟkōḷum, ētuvum, eṭuttuk kāṭṭum ikkūṟiya muṟaiyē naṭaipeṟṟiyalum eṉpatu, tolkāppiyam paṭittaṟiyappukum māṇavar muṉṉarē aṟinta toṉṟākaliṉ, ikkuṟippu vēṇṭaṟpāṟṟaṉṟu, mēlum mēlum, iṉṉōraṉṉa puḷḷi paṟṟiya piḻai nikaḻcci accut toḻiṟkaṇ iyalpē eṉpataṟku ivar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa ik kuṟippu nūlē pōtiya cāṉṟākum. oṉṟu kūṟutum, “āṭūu vaṟicol makaṭūu vaṟicol” (col.2) eṉṟa cūttirak kuṟippil varaṟpāṟṟākiya,  “civaṇi - teyvaccilaiyār civaṇiyeṉpatu viṉaiyeccamaṉṟu; oṭuviṉ poruṇmai tōṉṟa vantatu eṉkiṉṟār. avar koḷkaiyil accol oru col viḻukkāṭeṉṟu koḷka” eṉṟa kuṟippu, iccūttirak kuṟippil, “icaikkumaṉ” eṉpatupaṟṟic cēṉāvaraiyarum teyvaccilaiyārum kūṟiyavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum kuṟippiṉkīḻ, accākiyiruttal perum piḻaiyākum.
iṉi, “muṟṟuccol eccappeyar eṉpavākaliṉ,” eṉṟa toṭarkkaṇṇuḷḷa eccappeyar eṉpataṟku eñciya peyarai yuṭaiya colleṉpatu poruḷ. iṅku eṉmaṉār eṉṉuñ col eccappeyar eṉpatu avinayaṉār matameṉpatu “peyareñcu kiḷavi peyaroṭu muṭimē” (col. 425) eṉṟa cūttirattiṉaṭiyiluḷḷa teyvac cilaiyāruraiyāṉum, peyar viṉaiyummai (naṉ. 359) eṉṟa cūttirattiṉaṭiyiluḷḷa mayilai nātaruraiyāṉum naṉku viḷaṅkum” eṉṟār. eṉṟa iccāstiriyār, “eccappeyar viṉaiyeytu mīṟṟiṉum” eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttiram (356) niṉaiviruppiṉ, “eccamāy varum peyar” eṉṟu kūṟiya mayilai nātaruraiyātal, “eccamākiya peyar” eṉṟa caṅkara namaccivāyarurai yātal kuṟittiruppar; tuṇaiyiṉmaiyāl, tam aṟiviṟkeṭṭiya iv “veñciya peyaraiyuṭaiya col,” eṉṟār. muṭikkap paṭuvatum, muṭippatumākiya ellāp peyarccoṟkaḷum innilaiyil eccappeyarāmeṉpatu innaṉṉūṟ cūttirattālē viḷaṅkutaliṉ, ataṉaiyaṟiyātu, ataṉaip peyareccaṅ koṇṭu muṭiyum eccap peyariṉīkkutaṟku, “muṟṟuc col eccap peyar” eṉa vicēṭittiruppataṉaiyumaṟiyātu, āciriyar nakkīraṉār mutaliya paṇṭaiya uraiyāciriyarkaḷ, “muṟṟuc col eccap peyar koṇṭu muṭiyumākaliṉeṉpatu,” (iṟai. a. po.1) eṉa vaḻaṅkutalu nōkkātu, eṉmaṉār eṉpataṉai eccap peyareṉat tiriyakkoṇṭu, ataṉōṭu niṟṟaliṉṟi, āciriyar avinayaṉār, teyvaccilaiyār eṉṟa irupērilakkaṇa aṟiñar mēlum ik kuṟṟattai yēṟṟukiṉṟār. ikkuṟippuraiyeḻutum cāstiriyā roḻiya, ciṟṟilakkaṇam kaṟṟa tamiḻc ciṟuvaruḷḷum oruvarum kūṟārākaliṉ, avar tam uraikaḷai yām īṇṭuk kāṭṭutaluṟiṉ vāḷātu iṭam niramputalum, kaṭṭurai viritalumē payaṉām. “cummā kiṭanta caṅkai ūtik keṭuttāṉ āṇṭi” eṉpatu pōla, centamiḻil ivar veḷiyiṭṭa ik kuṟippai, ataṉōṭu niṟkaviṭātu, “ oru puttakattil eḻutappaṭiṉ, apputtakam tolkāppiyam paṭikkum māṇavarkkup payaṉpaṭum” (mukavurai) eṉṟu ivarkku ōtik keṭuttār cilar.
iṉi, “cuṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya taṉmaiyeṉṉum oruvaṉ eṉpatu cuṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya taṉmaiyaṉeṉum oruvaṉ eṉṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu” eṉpatu vēṟoru kuṟippu. itaṉkaṇ, cuṭṭeṉṉum peyar, ākupeyarāyt taṉmai kuṟittuṇarak kiṭakkum coṟṟoṭarai māṟṟi makkaṭcuṭṭē yeṉpatu makkaḷeṉṟu varaintu cuṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya taṉmaiyaṉeṉum poruḷ tarumāṟu iyaittaṟkuriyatōr iyaipēyiṉmaiyiṉ, “cuṭṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya taṉmai eṉṉum oruvaṉ” eṉpatē tiruntiya pāṭameṉka. eṉavē, kāraṇamiṉṟiyē tiruttaṅ koḷḷutalum tām vēṇṭiyavāṟu poruḷkoḷḷutalum piṟavum ceytoḻukutal īṇṭēpeṟap paṭutaliṉ, avvoḻukkam nūlārāycci yuṭaiyār māṭṭey talākāt tīyoḻukkam eṉpatu cāstiriyār maṉattuṭ kōṭaṟ pāṟṟu. ikkuṟippiṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa kuṟippukkaḷil tam uṇarvuk keṭṭiya ētu kūṟiṉavar, īṇṭuk kūṟāmai kuṟṟam eṉka.
iṉi, “icaikkum eṉpatu ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccam” eṉavarum iḷampūraṇa uraippakutiyai maṟuppār, “mēlum ataṉiluḷḷa icaikkumeṉpatu ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccam eṉṟa vākkiyam porunta villai” eṉṟu koṇṭamēṟkōḷai valiyuṟuttuvāṉ kāṭṭiya ētukkaḷ iraṇṭiṉuḷḷum mutalāva taṉaik kūṟumiṭattu aiyuṟṟuk kūṟuvār vāypāṭu tōṉṟa, “cūttirattukkum poruḷ colluṅkāl viṉaimuṟṟākavē coṉṉatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu” eṉkiṉṟār. iḵtu “ētuvuṭaimai niccayikkappaṭāmai” yākiya kuṟṟam eṉka.
iṉic cēṉāvaraiyattu, “eṉmaṉār” eṉṉum coṉmuṭipu paṟṟi varum maṟuppuraip pattiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa, “eṉmaṉāreṉpatu iṭarppaṭṭuḻic ciṟupāṉvārātu, nūluḷḷum cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuḷ ḷum payiṉṟu varutalāṉum” eṉa varum toṭariṉ “nūluḷḷum” eṉpuḻi “innūluḷḷum eṉṟa pāṭaṅkāṭṭi, atuvē poruntu” meṉkiṉṟār. iruvakaippaṭṭa pāṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉait tuṇintu kūṟaluṟuvār, poruntumāṟṟiṟku ētuvātal, eṭuttuk kāṭṭātal, poruḷātal kūṟutalē iyalpu. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum kūṟātu, vāḷātu, “poruntu” meṉṟoḻital poruntātu. nul eṉṟa viṭattuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷeṉṟāṟ pōlac cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ellā ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, “in nūl” eṉṟa viṭattut tolkāppiyam oṉṟumē koḷḷappaṭumākaliṉālum, col nilavumiṭam ivvoru nūlē yaṉṟi ellā nūlumākaliṉālum, “nūluḷḷum” eṉṟa pāṭamē koḷka.

23. maturaikkāñciyārāycci
1. nūliṉ karupporuḷ
muṉṉurai:
iṟṟaikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟaiya iraṇṭāyirattu irunūṟu yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar maturaiyiliruntu aracu purinta pāṇṭiyaṉ talai yālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum maṉṉa ṉukku, akkālattu viḷaṅkiya nallicaippulavar palaruṭ ciṟantārāya māṅkuṭi marutaṉār eṉpār pāṭittantatorupāṭṭu itu. itu naviṟoṟum nayam payakkum perunūlāya pattuppāṭṭiṉuḷ oṉṟu. itaṟku maturai kāñci yeṉpatupeyar. ēṉaiyavaṟṟai ārāycciyiṉ muṭivil kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.
maturaikkāñci yeṉpataṟku maturaiyiṉiṭattu oru araca ṉukkuk kūṟiya kāñci yeṉpatu poruḷām. kāñciyāvatu "vīṭupēṟu nimittamākap palvēṟu nilaiyāmai cāṉṟōr aṭaiyum kuṟippiṉatām." itaṉai āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār,
"kāñci tāṉē peruntiṇaip puṟaṉē
  pāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ paṉṉeṟi yāṉum
  nillā vulakam pulliya neṟittē" eṉṟaruḷiyuḷḷār.
itaṟku āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉakkut tuṇaiyillāta vīṭupēṟu nimittamāka, aṟam poruḷ iṉpameṉṉum poruṭ pakutiyālum, avaṟṟiṉ uṭpakutikaḷāṉa uyir, yākkai, celvam, iḷamai mutaliyavaṟṟālum nilaipēṟillāta ulakiyaṟ vāḻkkaiyaip poruntiyatōr naṉṉeṟiyiṉai yuṭaiyatu kāñci eṉpatām eṉpar.
eṉavē, aṟam poruḷ iṉpameṉṉum poruṭpakutiyum, avaṟṟiṉ uṭpakutiyāya uyir, yākkai, celvam, iḷamai mutaliyaṉavum nilaiyāmaiyum, annilaiyātaṉavaṟṟai koṇṭē nilaiyāya poruḷāya vīṭupēṟṟiṉaip peṟum. vakaikkum ik kāñci yeṉpatu taṉṉakattē koṇṭu viḷaṅkumeṉpatu peṟap paṭṭatu.
pāṭṭiṉ karupporuḷ:
pallāṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ uyarttuk kūṟum vakai, iv vulakiṉai yāṇṭa uyarntōṉākat tōṉṟiya kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉē! malaikaḷaiyum kāṭukaḷaiyum tamakku araṇākakkoṇṭa maṟaneṟi aracar palaraiyaḻippa, ataṉai taṉ valikaṇṭu añcit, tam nāṭukaḷai viṭṭakaṉṟaṉarāka; nāṭṭu nilaṅkaḷaiyellām koṇṭa pērutaviyum, polantār viḷaṅkum mārpum uṭaiya vaṭimpalam niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ vaḻittōṉṟalē! teṉṉavaṉāya irāvaṇaṉait tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉravāṟu peyarttavar kiṭṭutaṟ kariya valimikka toṉ mutukaṭavuḷar. akattiyarāvar; avarkkup piṉṉar, cāṉṟōr tokaiyuḷ oruvarāy vaitteṇṇutaṟku mēviya cālpuṟṟa aṭippaṟṟōy! cīrcāṉṟa uyarnta nelliṉ peyaraip peṟṟa pāliyūr eṉṉum cīriya ūrai veṉṟukoṇṭa veṟṟimikkōy! nalamcāṉṟa pēraṇikalaṅkaḷai yellōrkkum koṭukkum vaḷḷaṉmai mikka palavākiya kuṭṭanāṭṭiṉar velkōvē. mutuveḷḷilai yeṉṉum ūriluḷḷār aṉaivarum toṉṟu moḻintu toḻil kēṭkum vakai, talaiyālaṅkāṉa meṉṉumiṭattut taṉṉai yetirtta cēra cōḻarāya perunila maṉṉariruvar, titiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, erumaiyūraṉ, iruṅkōvēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ eṉpārāya kuṟunila maṉṉaraivar ākiya eḻuvaraiyum, leṉṉumāṟu kaṭitu parantu ceṉṟu, nāṭu muṟṟavum erivāykkup paṭumpaṭi veṉṟu, avar muracamuṅ kaikkoṇṭu, veṟṟik kuṟiyākak kaḷavēḷviyum vēṭṭa kollum valimikka pukaḻ vēntē! koṟkaiyūr vāḻnar virumpum poruna! teṉpuricaikkaṇ vāḻum paratavarkkup pōrttoḻil ceyalvallavaṉē! pakaivar nāṭṭait taṉṉāṭākkum poruṭṭu, ceyyappaṭṭa avaratu nāṭukaḷuṭ kōṭaṟpāṉmaiyavāṉa pakutikaḷiṟ pala yāṇṭukaḷ taṅki, avaṟṟaip paṇṭaiyiṉ takuvaṉavaṟṟaip purintu, vellum pōriṉum ciṟantu niṟkum kuricil!
kōvē! vaṭavēṅkaṭam, teṉkumari, kuṇakaṭal, kuṭakaṭal iv vellaiyiṭaippaṭṭa nilattuḷḷōr tammuṭaṉ tiṟam pala kūṟit tam mēval kēṭkumāṟu ceytu, veṟṟiyōṭu ceṟintu naṭakkum koṟṟavarkkellām nī, kōṉākum taṉmai pūṇṭuḷḷāy. aṉṟiyum nī, oru poyyāl uyarnilai yulakumuṟṟum āṇṭuḷ ḷōr nukarum amiḻtoṭu peṟa varuṅkālam amaiyiṉum, ap poyyiṉai aṟavē kaṭintu meymmai cērnta naṭpuc cey talaiyē yuṭaiyāy. muḻaṅkuṅkaṭal ellaiyāka, iṭaiyē parantu viḷaṅkum ulakattuḷḷārum, umparnāṭṭut tēvarum kūṭip pakaivarāyt tiraṇṭu variṉum, ap pakaivarkku añcip paṇintoḻukum pāṉmaiyiṉ mēṟkoḷḷāy, teṉpula meṉappaṭum teṉṉāṭṭu malaikaḷaṉaittum niṟaiyumvakai, vāṇaṉ eṉṉum cūraṉoruvaṉ vaitta viḻuniti peṟal nēriṉum, nī, piṟarkūṟum paḻi namakku varuvatāka eṉṉum karuttuṭaiyai yākāy. cīriya poruḷkaḷaip piṟarkkuk koṭuttalaiyē yuḷḷum niṉatu neñcōṭu pukaḻum virumpuvāyāka.
aṉṉāy! iṟṟaippōtu niṉveṟṟiyāṟ peṟappaṭu  vatum, piṉṉar niṉvaḻit tōṉṟuvārkku uḷatāvatu māya koṟṟam, niṉ kulattiṟkuriyatum mēṟṟicaik kaṭṭōṉṟuvatu māki, ellārāṉum toḻappaṭum paḻamaicāṉṟa piṟaittiṅkaḷ nāṭōṟum ciṟakkumāṟu pōla, avar tam vaḻivaḻiyāka mikuvatāka. niṉ pakaivaratu ākkam, kīḻtticaik kaṭṭōṉṟum niṟaiyiruḷait tarum mati nāḷum tēyntoḻital pōl nāṭōṟum keṭuva tāka. niṉṉiṭattuṇṭākiya māyaiyum iṉik keṭṭoḻivatāka. cēṭ pulameṅkum ceṉṟu viḷaṅkum niṉ cīriyapukaḻ orukālum keṭātu nilaipeṟuvatākum.
iṉi, nī āḷum in naṉṉāṭu pulavar pāṭum pukaḻuṭaiyatu itaṉ naṭuvaṇ, kāṇṭaṟkiṉiya ōviyattaik kaṇṭāṟpōlum kāṭciyamaintu viḷaṅkuvatu maturai yeṉṉum nalla ūr innakark kaṇ, nāḷaṅkāṭi, allaṅkāṭiyeṉum iru perum niyamaṅkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ nāḷaṅkāṭiyiṉ naṉantalai yeḻum ōcai,mēkaṅkaḷ paṭintu mukattalāṟ kuṟaitalum yāṟukaṇ niṟaintu pāytalāl mikutalumiṉṟikkaraiporutu oru taṉmaiyatāy olikkum munnīraiyoppa, palarum pōntu palakālum koḷḷutalāṟ kuṟaitalum, palarum mēṉmēluṅ koṇṭu vara koṇṭuvara mikutalumiṉṟi oru taṉmaittāy, kaṇam eṉappaṭuvatum, ēḻu nāḷkāṟum naṭattappaṭuvatumāya viḻāk kālattu vēṟōr iṭattillāta veṟṟi neruṅkum tirunāḷum, tīviṉai kaḻuvum tīviya nīrāṭṭum (tīrttamāṭal) taṉkaṭ koṇṭu ēḻā nāḷantiyil, kūṭuman nāṭṭumakkaḷ ceyyum pōrāra vārattai ottu icaikkum. allaṅkāṭiyil mikuntu eḻutarum ōcai, akaṉṟa ivvulakattup piṟanāṭṭu vaṇikar īṇṭuc ceyta pēraṇikalaṅkaḷaik koṇṭupōtal kāraṇamākak kāṟṟāl untappaṭṭuvarum tam pāymarakkalam koṇarnta palavēṟuvakaip paṭṭa paṇṭaṅkaḷaip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷi liṟakkuṅkāl eḻuvatum iṟakkuvā riṭuvatumāṉa kalleṉṉum ōcaiyaiyoppa niṟaiya iraimēyntu tam pārppukkaḷaiyuḷḷik kūṭunōkki mālaik kālattu mīḷum vēṟupal paṟavaiyiṉ pēricaiyai ottu imiḻum.
iṉi, avvūrkkaṇ, eḻunta ñāyiṟu, taṉ oḷḷiya cuṭart tiraḷiṉ ciṉamāya veppattaip pakalellām parappip piṉṉart taṇintu, appoḻutiṉaic cērakkoṇṭu kuṭamutaṟkuṉṟañ (attakiri) cērtalum, kalleṉṉum mālaikkālam, nayanta kātalarkku eytum mammar nōykoṇṭu pukuntu nīṅkum. akkālai, mutal yāmamuṅ kaḻiyum, piṉṉar kaṅkul yāmam eṉappaṭum iraṇṭām yāmamum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkum kaiyaṟu yāmamāya mūṉṟām yāmamum kaḻiya, iravukkālat tiṟutip pakutiyāya viṭiyal eytum.
avviṭiyalil, maṟaiyōtal payiṉṟa antaṇar, ammaṟai yiṉuṭ kūṟappeṟum vaṇakkappakuti, vāḻttuppakutikaḷai āyntu ōtuvar; yāḻvallōr, tāḷavaṟutiyai iṉitu nuṉittu, narampaiyum naṉku teṟittu marutappaṇṇicaippar; kāḻōr (parikkārarkaḷ) kaṭuṅkaḷiṟukaṭkuk kavaḷa mūṭṭuvar; neṭuntērp puravikaḷ panti pantiyāy niṉṟu, pulluṇāt teviṭṭum; palvakaip paṇṭaṅ kaḷum viṟkum kaṭaikaḷil, viṟpōr meḻukutalaic ceyvar; kaḷ viṟpōr, tam puḷitta kaḷḷiṉ vilaiyai vāṅkuvōrkku uraippar; kaṟpuṭaiya makaḷir iruḷ māyntu katirviriyum viṭiyal eytutalāl, atupōtu ceyaṟ pālāṉavaṟṟaic ceytaṟku virumpi, tam kaṇkaḷai veṟiyōṭap paṇṇi, viḷaṅkum miṉṉoḻuṅkai yokkum poṟcilampu, tam ceñcīṟaṭiyiṟ kiṭantu pulampap puṟam pōtuvar; avvamayam, tiṇ cuvarpaṭaitta, avaruṟaiyum nallillaṅkaḷiṉ kāḻoṉṟu katavaṅkaḷ, tiṟakkap paṭukaiyil, pēroliceyyum; kaḷḷuṇṭu kaḷippōr, veṟiyāl, taṭṭamaḻalaic coṟkaḷai (taṭaipaṭṭu varum coṟkaḷ) vaḻaṅka, kaḷḷuṇṇum paḻañcerukkāḷar tam muḻakkattil accoṟkaḷ muṉṉiṉṟeḻum; niṉṟēttum iyalpiṉarāya cūtarkaḷ vāḻtta, iruntēttum mākatar tam maṉṉaṉ pukaḻai nuvaluvar; vaitāḷikaḷ tattam tuṟaikku uriyaṉavaṟṟai muṟaiyē iṉitāka icaippar; nāḻikai kūṟuvōr nāḻikai aḷantu colluvar; paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam orupuṭaiyatira, ēṟukaḷ tammuḷ māṟupaṭṭu orupāl muḻaṅkum poṟimayirkkōḻikaḷ orucār vaikaṟaiyiyampa, orucār vaṉ ṭāḻṅkurukukaḷum, kāmuṟṟa aṉṉac cēvalum tattamakkuriya peṭaikaḷai yaḻaikkum; aṇimayilum tam ammeṉ peṭaiyiṉai aḻaippāṉ akavum; piṭiyoṭu kūṭiya peruṅkaḷiṟukaḷ muḻakkañceyya, karaṭi mutaliya valimikka vilaṅkukaḷ kūṭṭiluṟaiyum vayappuliyōṭu kalantu muḻaṅkum. nīlavāṉattiṉ kaṇ miḷarum miṉṉiṉ nuṭakkamum māṇiḻaiyu muṭaiya māvuṇṭu kaḷippu mītūrnta makaḷir tam kaṇavaroṭu pulantaṉarāy aṟutteṟinta paruttavaṭamāya ārattiṟ corinta muttukaḷōṭu, poṉṉurukkum neruppuc cintiya nilam pōla māṇikkam, marakatam, poṉ, maṇi mutalāyiṉavum paccai pākkum, putumaṇal paranta muṟṟattil viḻuntu kiṭattalākiya avaṟṟaiyum piṟa vāṭiya pūkkaḷaiyum vaṇṭuntēṉum moyttu ārkkum.
aṉṟiyum, ivvūr, vaḷaṅkeḻumiya arumpaṇṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭu, piṟartōḷ kuṟṟamām vakaip perutta peruntōḷ maṟavar palaraik keṭuppa avar puṟantantōṭuṅkāl iṭai pulamāya pōrkkaḷattu viṉaiyōrai yiḻantuniṟkum kōṭṭiyāṉai kaḷum, pakaippulattuk kavarappaṭṭa pāymā puravikaḷum, kāy ciṉaṅkaṉalum taṟukaṇmai mikka kūḷi (vēṭṭuvar) pakaivarūraic cuṭaveḻum oḷiyil nirain tuṟaiyum vīraraimāḷaveṭṭi ēnti yavēlē kōlākak koṇṭu ōṭṭikkoṇarnta āniraiyum, akanāṭu cūḻaniṟkum muḻumutalaraṇaṅkaṭ kamaittuḷḷa aṇaṅ kuṭaiya katavukaḷum, nāṭōṟum tamakkuc celvamikumāṟu kaiyāṟ ṟoḻutu vāyāl vāḻttip pakaṟkālattē piṟar tiṟaiyākak koṇṭuvara vanta cīriyakalaṅkaḷum, attaṉmaiya piṟavum kaṅkaip pēriyāṟu āyiramukamākak kaṭaliṉkaṭ parantatupōlap parantu niṟṟalāl oru vakaiyiṉum aḷantu muṭipukāṇṭal iyalāta takaimai yuṭaiyatāy, taṉ poṅkaḻakiṉaik kāṇpāṉ poṉṉa karum vaṟitākap puttēḷir vanteytum pukaḻpaṭaittatāy, nuṇṇiya aṟal paranta tuṟaitōṟum palvakaip pūkkaḷ ceṟinta taṇṭalaikaḷ cūḻap peṟṟatāy neṭuṅkālam, aṭippaṭṭirunta perumpāṇar irukkaiyum, maṇṇuḷḷa vaḷavu māḻnta nīlamaṇi pōlum nīr nirampiya kiṭaṅkum, viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiniṟkum palvakaip paṭaikaḷ amaikkappeṟṟa puricaiyum, mukiltavaḻum malaipōl nivanta māṭaṅkaḷōṭu teyvamuṟaiyum neṭunilai kaḷum, neypalakālu miṭutalāṟ karukiṉa tiṇṇiya katavukoṇṭu vaiyaiyāṟu iṭaiviṭātu ōṭumāṟupōla māvum māntarum iṭaiyaṟāta vāyilu muṭaiyatu; nāṉmāṭattāl malinta pukaḻ kūṭum perumpatiyumākum.
innakarkkaṇ,
'ciṉaitalai maṇanta curumpupaṭu centī
 oṇpūm piṇṭi aviḻnta kāvila
 cuṭar poḻin tēṟiya viḷaṅkukatir ñāyiṟ(ṟu)
 ilaṅkukatir iḷaveyil tōṉṟi yaṉṉa
 tamaṉiyam vaḷaiiya tāvil viḷaṅkiḻai
 nilamviḷak  kuṟuppa, mētakap polintu,
 mayilō raṉṉa cāyal, māviṉ
 taḷirē raṉṉa mēṉi, taḷirppuṟat (tu)
 īrkkiṉ arumpiya titalaiyar, kūreyiṟ(ṟu)
 oṇkuḻai puṇariya vaṇṭāḻ kātil
 kaṭavuṭ kayat(tu) amaṉṟa cuṭaritaḻt tāmarait
 tātu paṭu perum pōtu puraiyum vāṇmukat(tu)
 āytoṭi makaḷir'
poṟkalaṅkaḷil maṇanāṟum iṉiya pāṉattai yeṭuttu niṉakkuttaruvar. nī ataṉaiyuṇṭu, tuyilkoṇṭu, piṉṉarc celvattai niṉaintu iṉpuṟukiṉṟa paṟṟuḷḷam uṟakkattai yuṇarttutalāl, atu niṉaittup piṉṉar iṉiteḻuntu aracark kuriya kaṭaṉkaḷaik kaḻittuk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytalvēṇṭum. av vaḻipāṭṭai, vallōṉoruvaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu, eḻutik kai ceyyavumpaṭṭa oru pāvaiyiṉiṭattu teyvattaṉmai nikaḻntatupōlum vaṭivuṭaiyaṉāy nī viḷaṅkuvāyāka.
piṉṉar, naṟumaṇaṅkamaḻum cantaṉam pūcik katirviṭum muttu vaṭam tavaḻntu miḷirum niṉ akaṉṟamārpil, tēṉiṉamum piṟamūcuvaṉavum moyppa, kaḻuttiṉiṉṟun tāḻnta pūkkaḷ pala viraviya mālaiyum uṭaṉ kiṭantu tavaḻa, valikeḻumiya niṉ taṭakkaiyiṟ poṟṟoṭiyum, polañ ceyappolinta maṇikaḷ aḻuttiya mōtiraṅkaḷum cuṭarviṭṭu viḷaṅka, cōṟamaivuṟṟa nīruṭaik kaliṅkam oṉṟu (kañciyiṭṭup pāleṉa veḷukkac cey uṭai), mēlaṇiyap peṟum kalaṅkaḷāṟ polivumikum vakai yoḻuṅkupeṟa vuṭuttu, mikkuvarukiṉṟavōr yāṟṟu nīrp perukkaik kallaṇai yoṉṟu niṉṟu tāṅkiṉāloppat tam paṭaiyaik keṭuttu mēlvarukiṉṟa pakaivar paṭaip peruk kiṉai naṭuniṉṟu takaintu, avaraiyum aṟavē keṭutta pōrē virumpum paṭait talaivarkaḷ vāḷveṟṟi poruntiya niṉtāḷ veṟṟiyai vāḻtta, oṉṟā vulakattil ōṅku pukaḻē oṉṟum vaṇṇam niṟuviya māṟaṉ *mutalākap pakaivaṉ veṉṟu kollum tappāta vāḷēntiya iḷaṅkōcar īṟākap palarum cūḻvara, yāvarum pukaḻum pēraṇikalam pūṇṭa aimperum kēḷirumuṭpaṭa, maṟamikka kuṟunila maṉṉar palarum neṟi marapu vaḻāta niṉ vāymoḻi kēṭṭu ataṟkut takaniṟpa, poṟpuṭai pukaḻ viḷaṅkum aṟaṅ kūṟavaiyat tār neruṅki, niṉ aṟaviyalpaip pukaḻntētti, vāḻtta, "maṟavar, celvar, kuricilar, ūkkalar mutaliyōraiyum, peruñ cey yāṭavaraiyum tammiṉ; ēṉōrum tammiṉ" eṉac cilarai varaintu kūṟiyum, vāyiliṉ kaṇ takaiyāmal ivarpōlvārum paṭaiyāḷar mutaliyōrum 'varuka' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyum, kāṭcikkeḷiyaṉāy nāḷōlakkamirukkum pērakattiṉkaṇ vīṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
atukālai, 'pulavar, pāṇar, pāṭṭiyar, vayiriyar mutalā yiṉār varuka' eṉa aṉpukaṉiya vaḻaittu, avar tam cuṟṟattārāy, avarkaḷāṟ pōṟṟappaṭupavarāy varum pēriravalar aṉai vōrkkum nīr yāreṉṟum avarkaḷaik kēḷātu avar kāṭṭiṉa aḷavaikkoṇṭu, koṭiñci neṭuntērkaḷ yāṉai yoṭuṅ koṭuttu, vēṟṟup pulattiliruntu koṇarappaṭṭa arum poruḷkaḷai yāvarkkumaḷittu, ataṉūṭē, nāṭṭil vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷaip perukkip purattal vēṇṭumātaliṉ, naṟ poruṇūāṟ kaḷaik kacaṭaṟakkaṟṟut teḷivāyāka! kaṟṟavaṟiviṉāl, niṉpukaḻē evvulakattum niṉṟu nilavappaṇṇuvāyāka! maṇṭilamākkaḷ, taṇṭattalaivar mutalāya cuṟṟattārkku naṭuvē, munnīr nāppaṇ tōṉṟum ñāyiṟu pōlavum, palakalaikaḷaik kaṟṟōriṭaip paṉmīṉ naṭuvaṇ viḷaṅkum tiṅkaḷ pōlavum, nī polivukūr cuṟṟattōṭu polintu iṉitu vāḻvāyāka!
iṅṅaṉam, tampukaḻ eṅṅaṇum paravum vakai malartalai yulakiṉait tammēvaliṉ naṭatti, makkaṭkuriya maṉaṉuṇarviṉmai pāl piṟappaṟa muyalātu payaṉiṉṟi māṇṭōr, karaiyaip porutu iraṅkik kaṉaiyum irumunnīrttiraiyiṭum nuṇmaṇa liṉum palarāvar. appayaṉiṉmaiyālē nīyum avvāṟu kaḻiyalākātu eṉṟu ivvāḻviṟ peritāyiruppatōr poruḷai yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ; āyiṉum, aṇṇalē! aḵtu eṉṉāl eḷitiṟ kāṭṭalaritu. ataṉai, ellāvulakaiyum taṉ cevvāyiṟṟantu kāṭṭiya māyōṉpōṉṟa, nalla yākaṅkaṭkuk kūṟiya tuṟai yaṉaittum muṉṉarē muṭittuviṭṭa tollāṇai nallāciriya rām tattuvavuṇarvāṉ meypporuḷuṇarntu vīṭṭiṉpa nukarccikāṇum āciriyar palar piṉpu kūṭiṉa kantaḻiyāy koḷḷai yai  avariṭattē peṟṟu nukarnta pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉaṉ, palyāka cālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti yoppa nīyum nallāciri yariṭattē kēṭpāyāka! kēṭṭu, nī, avvāciriyariṭattup peṟum kantaḻiyiṉ viyappum, piṉpu nī peṟṟamaiyum amaitiyum, pukaraṟu ciṟappiṉavāy eytum niṉ talaimaikaḷ yāvum cāṉṟōr palarāl tam mavaiyiṟ collappaṭṭu viḷaṅkum peruma! nallūḻiyuḷ "ittuṇaikkālam irutti" eṉap pālvarai teyvattāl varaiyāka aṟutiyiṭappeṟum nāḷ muḻuvatum nī iṉitu pēriṉpam nukarntiruppāyāka! ataṉai nukarātu aimpoṟikaṭkum muṉṉiṟkap paṭuvaṉavāya innukar poruḷkaḷōṭu niṉakku eṉṉavuṟa vuṇṭu! niṉṉiṭattuṇṭāya māyai iṉik keṭuvatāka!! aṇṇalē! nī neṭitu vāḻka!!!
 2. maturaic ciṟappu
maturaikkāñci yeṉṉum iccīriya pāṭṭiṉ perum pāṉmaiyāṉa pakutikaḷ maturaiyiṉ ciṟappaiyum, ataṉaic cūḻavuḷḷa nāṉilaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷaṉaiyum, ataṉait talainakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉaiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ākaliṉ avaṟṟaimaṭṭil ivvārāycciyiṉ ippakutiyil veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ.
1.maturaiyāṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉ: ivvulakam mikka parappum uyariyamuṭikaḷ koṇṭa malaikaḷum taṉpāṟ koṇṭatu. malai muṭikaḷil tēṉiṟāl tūṅkum māṇpum, yāṟukaḷ tōṉṟip paṭarum ciṟappum, kāṉañceṟintu niṟkum kaviṉum kāṇpār kaṭpulaṉaik kavarum kāṭciyiṉavām. ikkāṭciyamainta ulakiṉai, ippāṭṭuṭaittalaivaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ āṭcipurintu purantu varunāḷ, kāṟṟu valamāka vicumpiṉkaṇ cuḻaṉṟu tiriya, ataṉāl  maḻai vēṇṭuṅkālatut tappātu peytu utava, nāṟṟicaiyum viḷai nalaṉum piṟavum mikaipaṭappeṟṟaṉa; toṭuppiṉ, vitaitta oṉṟu āyiramāka viḷaiyum nilavaḷañciṟantatu; viḷainilaṉum maraṅkaḷum tampālām payaṉaip palluyirkkuṅ koṭuttalai mēṟkoṇṭu, taḻaittaṉa; aṅkaṇ vicumpil, āṅkāṅ kumiḷirum nāṇmīṉ tattam nilaitiriyātu koṭpuṟ ṟoḻukiṉa; ceññāyiṟu pakaṟpōtu ceytum, veṇṭiṅkaḷ iravu ceytum, maitīrntu kiḷarntu viḷaṅkiṉa; makkaṭku nōytaruvaṉavāya paciyum piṇiyum ilavāki nīṅka, nōkku viḷaṅkum vaciyum vaḷaṉum perukiṉa; eṇṭicaiyiṉum iruntu ulakiṉpoṟai yēṟkum vayakkaḷiṟukaḷ aṟavum polivupeṟṟu, vaḷarcciyum valiyum oruṅkuvāyppa, ulakāḷ maṉṉaratu ceṅkōṉmai yāṉ, appoṟaiyiṉ varuttam pēṇātu mēmpāṭupeṟṟaṉa.
innalaṅkaḷaittarum āṭcikkuk kaṇṇeṉa vakutta māntar vāymoḻiyāl pukaḻ niṟaintavar, poyyaṟiyāp polivum peṟṟavar; ivar vāḻunterukkaḷ palarōṭiruntu palanāḷuṇṇiṉum taṇṭāta mikuvaḷaṉum irumaruṅkum maḻaiyeṉa maruḷum māṭampiṟaṅkutalāl, kāṇpavar kaṇṭu taṇṭāk kāṭciyum peṟṟaṉavām. ivarkaḷ amaiccut toḻilpūṇṭu āṭcit tuṟaiyil utaviceyya, pala nallūḻikaḷ tamakku aṭippaṭṭu naṭakka, ivvāṭcinalaṉaip pala veḷḷam eṉum eṇṇiyaṟ peyaraik koṇṭa kālamellām makkaḷ mīkkūṟa, ippāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉatu muṉṉōr ulakiṉai ōṅkutirai yolikkum munnīrē varampāka niṟka āṇṭaṉar eṉpatu.
2. vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ: ivaṉ "nilantanta pērutavip polantārmārpiṉ neṭiyōṉ" eṉac ciṟappikkap peṟukiṉṟāṉ. peṟavē, ivaṉ, nāṭṭiluḷḷa ēṉaiyaracar palarum taṉ tuṉṉarun tuppiṉaikaṇṭu añcit tam nāṭaṉaittum viṭṭuakaṉṟārāka, *avaṟṟaik koṇṭu taṉ kīḻvāḻvārkkuk koṭukkum vaḷḷaṉmaiyum, pōrvaṉmaiyum talaiciṟantavaṉ eṉpatu peṟalākum. eṉiṉum ivaṉ, viḻumiya vaḻiyum, viḷaṅkiya ōṭaiyum, kaṭuñciṉamum, kaṭām kamaḻutalāl aḷaṟupaṭṭa ceṉṉiyum, varaimaruḷum tōṟṟamumuṭaiya viṉainaviṉṟa pōr yāṉaikaḷ pala ciṉañciṟantu, pōrkkaḷattu vīraraikkoṉṟu tirintuḻakkavum, kutiraittiraḷ pakaippulanōkkip pāytara, āṇṭeḻum centukaḷ akalvāṉattu veyilai maṟaippavum, varavum parikaḷ pala piṇaitta tiṇṇiya tērkaḷ kāṟṟeṉṉa viraintu koṭpuṟavum, vāṭpōriṉ mikka maṟaṅkoṇṭu tiriyum maintar palar tōṇmuṟaiyāl vīṟumuṟṟavum, iruperuvēntarāya cēracōḻaroṭu vēḷir palarum cāyntōṭap porutu, avaraic ceruveṉṟum amaiyāmal, uyarntilaṅkum aruvikaṉmikka maivaraiyulakattu maṉṉar palaraiyum veṉṟuyarntu, taṉṉoṭu māṟukoṇṭa pakai maṉṉar tam nāṭukaḷaip palavākac citaṟuṇṇap piḷanta ciṟappum mikkavaṉ eṉpatu pōtantatu.
3. maturaiyiṉ vaḷam
nāṭṭuc ciṟappu:- vāṉam kuṇakaṭaliṉ nīrai mukantu ceṉṟu kuṭamalaivaraiyilmuṟṟi, avvaraiccāraliṉ vaḷarntōṅkum kaḻaikaḷ nilai taḷaravum, vāḻum kaḷiṟṟiṉamum añci alamaravum, urumēṟṟōṭu parantu, iravum pakalum oḻiviṉṟip perumaḻai peytatāka, āṅkāṅku, kallappaṭṭirukkum kavalaikkiḻaṅkiṉ aḻakiya kuḻumpukaḷil (kuḻikaḷil) nīr niṟaintu mikutippaṭṭu aruviyeṉa vaḻintōṭa, innīrmikutiyait tāṅkātu tiraṇṭa puṉal nīttam, cenniṟaṅkoṇṭu, kuṇakaṭal nōkkip parantu cellum, avvaḻi, iṭaiyiluṟum ēri kuḷam mutaliyaṉa nīr nirampap peṟa, avaṟṟiṟkīḻ vayaṅkum kaḷiṟu niṟpiṉum maṟaikkum uyarccicāṉṟa katirviḷaiyum cenneṟkaḻaṉikaḷum, oḷirum ilañcikaḷum (maṭukkaḷ) nīr vaḷam mēmpaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum. aṭaiyum, muṭṭāḷum ko ṇṭatāmarai, kaṭkamaḻum neytal, vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻum nīlam, vaṇṭiṉam paṭiyum mellilai koṇṭa āmpal mutaliyaṉavum, maṟṟum piṟa curumpiṉam iṟaikoḷḷum pūkkaḷum niṟaintu maṇaṅkamaḻntu, tēṉārnta curumpāṭat tikaḻkiṉṟa poykaiyiṉkaṇṇum, iṭaiyiṭaiyē paṭarntu niṟkum vaḷḷaik koṭikaḷait taḷḷi, vayamuṟṟa mīṉkaḷai valaiñarkaḷ tam valaikaḷāṟ piṭittuk koṉṟu kuvippar. atupōtu, avvaḷḷaik koṭikaḷiṟṟaṅki yuṟaṅkiyirunta kampuṭ kōḻi paṟantōṭuṅkāl eḻum ōcaiyum, koṭumuṭikaḷ mikka mīṉvalai vīcum valaiñar, ammīṉkaṭku vilaikūṟik kaḷikkum ōcaiyum orupakkam eḻuntu ciṟakkum. orupāl, karumpukaḷai āṭṭiyaṭum entiraccālaiyiṉōcaiyum, vēṟorucār kaḷaip paṟippōr ceyyum ōcaiyum, avvayalarukē, aḷḷaṟkaṇ valiyaṟṟu vīḻnta mutumaimikka pakaṭukaḷai, eḻupputaṟkāka, kaḷḷuṇṭakaḷamar, avaṟṟikku uṟṟaviḻumam teriyātavāṟu peyarkkum āravāramum ōṅkum. taḻaitta pakaṉṟai mutaliyaṉa mikkuṟum kaḻaṉikaḷil vittiya nel muṟṟiyatāka, ataṉai aṟuttaṟkaṇ, avvaṉkaiviṉaiñar ceyyum ōcaiyum, iṉkural toṭutta vācciyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ maḻaivaḷaṉaṟāt taṇṇiya paraṅ kuṉṟattil naṭaipeṟum viḻākkaḷil eḻum nallōcaiyum, putunīr viḻāvayartaṟ poruṭṭup pērāravārattuṭaṉ cellum makaḷir tam kōtaiyai maintar puṉaiyavum, ammaintariṉ taṇṇiya tārait tām puṉaiyavumākak kūṭiyāṭum nīrāṭṭōcaiyu mākaveḻum ivvōcaikaḷōṭu, kurukukaḷ mēlē cūḻa, vīṭukaḷiṉaruku niṟkum maraṅkaṭōṟum; niraiyē kōttu uṇakkap peṟum vaṟṟaṉ mīṉkaḷ acaiyum pāṇarkuṭiyiruppuk kaḷil, avar pāṭalāl eḻum ōcai muṟṟum kalantu, viṇpaṭarntu, āṇṭuvāḻvārkku iṉiya icaiyiṉpam nalkum. itu maruta nilavaḷaṉ.
iṉi, mullai nilavaḷaṉ varumāṟu: itu kāṭum, kāṭu cārnta nilamum poruntiyatu. īṇṭu, kāṉavar orupakkal niṉṟu tiṉaikoyvar. orucār eḷḷiḷaṅkāy muṟṟiniṟpa, iruntāḷ varaku taṉ muṟṟiya katirkāṭṭi aḻakuceyya, āḻnta viḻikaḷil maṇikaḷ kiṭantu miḷira, pēraḻaku vāynta navvimāṉkaḷ eḻun tōṅkiya akkāṭṭiṉkaṇ, maṭappam poruntum kaṇṇiṉai yuṭaiya piṇaimāṉōṭu, tam ciṟutalaiyaiyuyartti pāṭiyāṭiyukaḷum. cempoṟṟukaḷ, parantamaṇaliṉ mēṟpuṟattē yaṭaintu nalloḷi parappum. parappivaittāṟ pōlum pāṉmaiyavāyp pāṟaikaḷ, oḷḷiya koṉṟai paranta nīḻalil, iṉiya kāṭciyiṉai nalkum. nīlamaṇiyaṉṉa pacumpayirkaḷiṉ mēl murukkuṇṭa mucuṇṭaiyiṉ oḷḷarumpukaḷōṭu, mullaiyiṉ veḷḷiyotta voṇpūkka ḷutirntu parantu, aḻaku ceyya, avaṟṟiṉarukē, vallōṉ oruvaṉ taiiya (eḻutiya kūttayar veṟikkaḷamoppa, melliya avalkaḷil teḷinta nīr niṟaintu, nīlamaṇiyeṉat tikaḻum neytal, iṭai maṇan toyyiṟ koṭiyoṭu māṟupaṭa malarntu vayaṅkum.
iṉic cēyōṉ mēya maivarai yulakameṉappaṭum kuṟiñci yil, varaiccāralkaḷiṟ kiṭakkum perum pāṟaikaḷ, veḷḷaruvikaḷ pala vīḻtalālum, nīrttāraikaḷiṉ uṟaippālum karuniṟaṅ koṇṭu añcuvaru kāṭcipayappa, iṭaiyiṭai ceḻippuṟṟut taḻaitta paiṅkūḻum piṟavum āmā mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum pulluṇā vaḷikkum poṟpu viḷaṅkum. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa tiṉaip puṉaṅkaḷiṟ paṭiyum kiḷimutaliya puṭkaḷaik kāṉavar oppum ōcaiyum, tuṭavaikaḷiṟ ceḻittu niṉṟu, palamaṇikaḷaiyokkum aḻakiya pūkkaḷ niṟaintu viḷaṅkum avaraiyiṉ naṟuntaḷirai mēyavarum avvāmā mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟavarkaḷ kaṭitalāleḻum ōcaiyum, malaimicaiyuṟaiyum akkuṟicci makkaḷ poykkuḻitoṭṭuk kēḻal mutaliyavaṟṟaip piṭittaṟ poruṭṭu, ataṉ vāyai mēlāka mūṭivaippa, avvaḻivantakēḻal ataṉkaṇ vīḻtalum, ataṉaik kollum veṟṟiyāka ceyyum āravāramum, karuṅkāl vēṅkaiyiṉ naṟumpūk koṭiyum kuṟamakaḷir 'pulipuli' eṉṟu kūṟiyāṭum ōcaiyum, im malaiccāralkaḷil māṟimāṟi yolik kum. aṉṟiyum iccāraṅkaḷ, kuṟuṅkatirtāṅkum tēraiyum, neṭuṅkālaiyuṭaiya aiyaviyum, aivaṉameṉṉum veṇṇellu mākiya viḷainalam ciṟantu iñci, mañcaḷ, miḷaku mutaliyaṉavum piṟavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ pala mikuntu viḷaṅkum.
 iṉi, aruvikaḷum, avaṟṟāluḷatā maḻakumillāta pala malaikaḷ āḻnta viṭākaṅkaḷ koṇṭuniṟpatu pālai. niṟain teḻum cūṟaikkāṟṟu, ūkampul mutaliyaṉa ularntu kiṭakkum kāṭṭuḷ, vaikkōlaic cāṭuvatupōl avaṟṟaic cāṭiyalaittu avviṭākaṅkaḷiṉ mōticcērttu aṇaittu aṭaṅkum kāṟṟu mōta, alaipaṭuṅkaṭaliṉkaṇ eḻum pērōcai yotta peru muḻakkam accūṟāvaḷiyiṉ payaṉāka yeḻum. innilattuk kāyum veyil, niḻalumtaṉ uruviḻakkun taṉmaittāya vēṉil veppa muṭaiyatu. ikkālattil, malaiyaruku kiṭakkum curattiṉ kaṇ, vēṭṭuvaviḷaiñar palar cilaiyēnti āṟalaikkum kaḷvaraik kāttaṟ poruṭṭuk karantuṟaivar. ivarkaḷuṟaiyum maṉaiyakaṅkaḷ taḻai vēyappaṭṭum, ivaratu paṭukkaikaḷ māṉtōl parappap peṟṟum irukkum. potuvāka, ivarkaḷ taḻai viraviya kaṇṇi cūṭumiyalpum, kaṭuñcoṟ kūṟum kāyciṉamumuṭaiyar.
iṉik kaṭaṟcārntatāya neytal vaḷaṉ varumāṟu: kaṭaṟṟuṟai kaḷil, perunīrōccum marakkalamīkāmar palaruṭaṉ kūṭi, iṭama kaṉṟa yavaṉam mutaliya piṟanāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu koṇarnta kutiraikaḷuṭaṉ, muḻaṅkuṅ kaṭaltanta viḷaṅkukatir muttamum, aram pōḻntaṟutta ilaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaikaḷum, paratar (ceṭṭikaḷ) mutaliyōr koṇṭutanta palavēṟuvakaik kūlaṅkaḷum orupuṭai malaiyum kaṭalumeṉa mikkuttōṉṟa, vēṟōr pakkattil, iruṅkaḻikaḷil viḷainta veḷḷuppum, karuppukkaṭṭiyoṭu kūṭṭip porikkap paṭṭa puḷiyum, parantōṅkiya kāṉalil vaṉkaittimilar (mīṉ piṭikkum paṭaku uṭaiyavarkaḷ) tām koṇarnta koḻumīṉ kaḷait tuṭiyiṉ kaṇpōl uruṇṭa tuṇṭukaḷāka aṟutta uṇakkiya karuvāṭukaḷum ēṟṟappaṭṭa nāvāykaḷ pala niṉṟu acaiyum polivu viḷaṅkum. iṅṅaṉam, ivai nāṭōṟum muṟaiyē mikum peruvaḷam payiṉṟatu in neytal.
ivvaṇṇam aintiṇaivaḷaṉum amaintu aḻakupeṟṟu, muḻavu muḻaṅkun tirunāḷ naṭaipeṟum neṭunterukkaḷum, tuṇaṅkai, kuravai mutaliya kūttukkaḷ nikaḻum parattaiyar cēriyum iṭaiyaṟāp putuvaruvāyum koṇṭa pala ūrkaḷum, avaṟṟuṭ pala kuṭittiraḷum mikuntiruttalāl, ippāṇṭināṭu pāṭal cāṉṟa paṇpuṭaiya nāṭāyiṟṟu.
nakaracciṟappu: itu perumpā ṇirukkaiyum, maṇi nīrk kiṭaṅkum, palpaṭaippuricaiyum vaiyaipōlum vaḻakkuṭaiya vāyilumuṭaiyatu. ivaṟṟuṭ perumpāṇirukkaiyiṉ nalam.
perumpā ṇirukkaiyāvatu perumpāṇar eṉṉum oru vakaiccātiyār vāḻum iṭam. itu, vaiyaittuṟaitōṟum palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa pūntōṭṭaṅkaḷ cūḻappeṟṟu avaṟṟi ṉiṭaiyil neṭuṅkālamāka iruntuvarum toṉmai ciṟantatu. aṉṟiyum itu, yāṟṟiṉ aṭaikaraiyiliruttalāl kāvum poḻilum mikku, uyar cimayattu niṉṟu iyaṅkivarum nīr koḻippa varum nuṇṇiya veṇmaṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷ tammēṟ paṭinta pūkkaḷāl naṟumaṇam vīca, muḻaṅkukāl porutalāl māvicumpu taṭavum maraṅkaḷacaiya, mayilkaḷ akavi āravārippa, mucukkalaikaḷ tāvum komparil avaṟṟiṉ mantikaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭayarum peruñ ciṟappuṭaiyatu. karaiyiṉiyalpu iṟṟāka, yāṟṟu nīroḻukku, tātu cūḻnta kōṅkam pūvum vēṟu piṟa malarkaḷum parantu, orukōtai (pūmālai) yoppa oḻuki yaḻaku ceyyum.
maṇinīrk kiṭaṅku: innūṟporuḷ nikaḻnta kālattukku muṉṉar māṉaviṟal vēḷ eṉṉumoru kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ aḻum pil eṉṉum ūruṭaiyaṉāy viḷaṅkiṉaṉ. avvūr nila vaḷamum poruḷ vaḷamum mikuntatu. itaṉ vaḷattaik kāṇappukum oruvaṉatu kāṭci vēṟoru nilattaiyum viḷainalaṉaiyum kāṇṭaṟkuc cellāteṉpar. ittakaiya aḻumpilai yotta nāṭiḻantavarkaḷum, celvaṅ koḻutta ceḻumpatikaḷiṭattavāya kuṭiyiḻantavarkaḷumāyt toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum ceṟṟaṅkalanta valimai tammait tūṇṭutalāl etirppāṉ vanta aṇṇal yāṉaiyum aṭupōrt toḻilumuṭaiya vēntarpalar uḻiñaip pōrceytaṟku vantārāka, avar neñciṟ kiṭanta māṟupā ṭaṉaittaiyum pōkki, tām tam iṉṉicaimuracu mutaliyavaṟṟai iṭaippulattē viṭṭōṭac ceytu avar mutuku kāṇum vakaiyiṟ kāttuniṟkum mikka āḻamuṭaiyatu akkiṭaṅku.
vāyil: maḻaitavaḻum malaiyeṉa uyarntōṅkiya māṭaṅ kaḷāṟṟikaḻntu, mikka mutumaiyum valimaiyuṅ koṇṭu niṟkum innakarkkaṇvāyil, aṇaṅku vāḻtaralāl aiyaviyum neyyum appiya neṭunilaiyum, tiṇṇiya katavaṅkaḷum uṭaiyatāy, iṭaiyaṟā nīrpperukkiṉaik koṇṭu ōṭum vaiyaiyāṟṟiṉaip pōla māntarum māvum iṭaiyaṟātu vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉai yuṭaiyatāy viḷaṅkum.
iṅṅaṉam vaḻakkiṭaiyaṟātu viḷaṅkum vāyil kaṭantu uḷḷakam nōkkuvārkkuk kaṭpulaṉāvatu yāṟu kiṭantaṉṉa akaṉṟa neṭunteruvēyākum. ataṉ irumaruṅkum, maṇṭapam, kūṭam, tāykkaṭṭu, aṭukkaḷai mutaliyaṉavākap palavēṟu vakaikaḷ viraviya māṭaṅkaḷ vāṉakam mūḻki niṉṟu uyarcci kāṭṭa, avaṟṟiṉ cāḷaraṅkaḷ, ammēṭaiyilē vīcukiṉṟa melliyateṉṟalai varavaḻaikkum. teruviṉkaṇ, viḻāvayartal virumpi nakar mākkaḷ eṭutta pērōcai orupuṭaiyeḻuntu atira, avvōcai kēṭṭu makiḻum vēṟoru cārār pala iṉṉiyam iyampuvar. avaṟṟiṉ ṟiraṇṭavōcai nīrāṭṭiṭaiyeḻum muḻakkattai yokkum. niṟka, immāṭaṅkaḷiṉ mēṉilai yiṟṟikaḻum neṭuṅkoṭikaḷum, viḻā virumpiyeṭutta viyaṉ koṭikaḷum, vēṟṟu vēntarai vēṟalāleṭutta nalam peṟu koṭikaḷum, nīr olittatupōla mikkolittuvanta vēṟ paṭaiyaip pulavuppaṭak koṉṟu, uṭaṉ mēlneruṅkiya yāṉaip paṭaiyaiyum aṟavē cāyttup pukaḻceytu vīṟuṟṟu eṭutta viṟalcāṉṟa naṉkoṭikaḷum, peruvarai maruṅkiṉ vīḻum aruvi pōl nuṭaṅkittōṉṟa, kaḷviṟpō reṭuttakoṭi yeḻuntu acaiyum kāṭci, anniyamattai naṉku aṇiceyyum.
iṉi, anniyamaṅkaḷuḷ, kantu nīttu uḻitarum kaṭām poḻiyāṉai, kāleṉakkaṭukkum tēr, iṭumayirppuravi, kaḷḷār kaḷamar uṭpaṭa nālvakaittāya paṭaitiraṇṭu muṟaiyē cella, naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pūkkaḷ pala pūntaṭṭukkaḷiṟ koṇṭu niṟpavarum, oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu māṟupaṭum vakaiyiṟ palapaṭaviritta mālaikaḷai ēnti niṟpavarum, navamaṇikaḷ, poṉ, cantaṉam, karuppūram mutaliyavaṟṟaic cēra iṭittut tukaḷceytu, pūntātu oppat tūymaippaṭuttiya cuṇṇattai uṭaiyavarum, paciya karuṅkāli yaic cīvik kāycci yeṭutta kaḷippoṭu, iṉiya nīrmaikoṇṭa pākkukkaḷaik kalantukoṇṭu, avaṟṟoṭu veṟṟilaiyum cēravaittu viṟpavarum, caṅkaic cuṭuṅkāṟ peṟalākum cuṇṇāmpu uṭaiyavarumākiya palavēṟu vakaippaṭṭa makkaḷ, irumaruṅkum vantu kalanta pakaippulam pōṉṟu viḷaṅkutalāl, tam iṉṉuyirkku añci, veytuyirttu, ēṅkiyiruntu, piṉṉar annāṟpaṭaiyum nīṅka, māṭa nīḻalilē pala iṉṉiyaṅkaḷ kaṟaṅkac cemmāntiruppar.
niṟka, maṉaiyakaṅkaḷil maṭappamum nōkkiṉārai varuttum ceyirppu muṟṟa kaṇkaḷum, aḻakuṟappaṭarntu tōṉṟum māmai yum, niraikeṭātu vārntu kūriyavāy ilaṅkum eyiṟuṭaiya cevvāyum, mūṅkil pōlum tōḷum, viḷaṅkum oḷikoṇṭa kaivantiyum *toyyil poṟitta cuṇaṅkupūtta iḷamulaiyum, maipōlum kariya moytta kūntalum, mayilaṉṉa cāyalum, maṭappamum uṭaiya makaḷir palarum, tammaik kōlañceytu, melleṉak kaiyeṟintu naṭantu, kāmanukarcciyaṉṟip piṟitoṉ ṟiṉaiyuṅ kallāta iḷaiyōruṭaṉ kūṭikkaḷikkumpaṭi caṅkoppa naraitta kūntalaik kōtip piṉṉē muṭiyiṭṭup poliyum toṉmutu peṇṭir palar, kāmaru paṇṇiyaṅkaḷ pala "puṭaiyamai polinta vakaiyamai ceppu" kkaḷil vaittu, maṇam kamaḻum pūkkaḷaiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu ceṉṟu maṉaitōṟum ulāvi viṟpaṉai ceyvar.
iṉi, immaṉaiyakam kaṭantu celliṉ, oruvaṉ kāṇalāvaṉa kaṭavuṭpaḷḷi, antaṇar paḷḷi mutaliyaṉavām. avaṟṟuṭ kaṭavuṭ paḷḷiyeṉpatu pauttappaḷḷiyām. tiṇṇiya katirkaḷait teṟikkum pēraṇikalaṅkaḷ palapūṇṭu, kāmarupēriḷam peṇṭir tām virumpikkūṭip pōṟṟum tam kaṇavaruṭaṉ, tātaṇintu viḷaṅkum tāmaraip pōtaik kaiyiṟ piṭittuc cellutaloppa oḷḷiya ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷait tam kaiyiṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu, ellōrumākat tiraṇṭu ippaḷḷikaḷai yaṭaintu, pūvum pukaiyuṅ koṇṭu, paḷḷik kaṭavuḷaip paravit toḻutu vaṇaṅkuvar.
antaṇar paḷḷiyāvatu ekkālamum maṟaimuṭivaiyē pārttoḻukalāṟṟum pāṉmaiyar uṟaiyum viḻumiya viṭamām. ivarkaḷ maṟaināṉkaṉuḷ, ciṟappillāta atarvamaṟai nīṅkalāka, ēṉaicciṟappuṭai maṟaimūṉṟaiyum tamakkup poruḷ viḷaṅkum vakaiyiṟ pāṭutaluṭaiyavar. aṉṟiyum, ivar viḻumiya cīr payappatāya vēḷvi yoḻukkatti līṭupaṭṭu niṟpavar. ivaratu neñcam aṟaneṟi piḻaiyāta aṉpu niṟaintu, uyar nilaimaiya tāṉa umparulakaiyum ivaṇ niṉṟē yeytum koḷkaimēṟ koṇṭu, viḷaṅkum viḻuppamuṭaiyatu. itaṉāl, ivar, innāl vakai nilaṅkaḷamainta ulakattil opparum mikkārumilarāki, orutāmāki uṟaikiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cīvaṉ muttarāyirukkum ivar virumpiyuṟaiyum iṭamē ippaḷḷi. itu malaiyoṉṟai uḷveḷiyāka vāṅki iruppiṭamākkiṉāṟ pōṉṟa tiṇmaiyum perumaiyum amaintatākum.
ivaṟṟōṭu uṭaṉeṇṇappaṭuvaṉavāyp pala aṟiñar cēkkaikaḷ (amaṇappaḷḷikaḷ) arukē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai, cempiṉāṟ ceytamaittāṟpōlum cevviyacuvarkaḷ cūḻntavai; nōkkuvār kāṭcivicaiyum tavirkkum vakaiyil mēlēpuyarntu, kuṉṟaṅkaḷ pala aṇukkattē niṟaintu tōṉṟuṅ kāleḻunta kāṭciyiṉaip peṟṟu, viḷaṅkuvaṉavām. iṅku, tātuṇṭa vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻvantu muralum tēṉārum pūkkaḷum naṟumaṇam kamaḻum pukaiyum koṇarntu cāvakar mutaliyōr vaṇaṅkip paravuvar; antak 'kayam kuṭaintaṉṉa vayaṅkuṭai'k kōyiliṉ kaṇṇirukkum periyōr, vāṉamum nilamum oruṅkē muḻutum uṇarum cāṉṟa koḷkaiyiṉaiyum, akkoḷkaikkut taḷartal illaiyāyc cāyāta tiṇṇiya yākkaiyum amaiyap peṟṟavar. mēlum, *ivar pulaṉkaṇmēṟ cellukiṉṟa avāvain taṉaiyum avitta pērāṟṟal paṭaittamaintavar; ceṉṟa kālamum varūum amaiyamum, iṉṟu ivaṇ tōṉṟiya (nikaḻkālam) oḻukkamoṭu naṉkuṇarum matinuṭpam nūlōṭuṭaiyar. ivaratu cēkkaikaḷil pala vaṭaṅkaḷāl amainta nūluṟikaḷ toṅkum. avaṟṟil ivaratu kuṇṭikaikaḷ vaikkap peṟṟirukkum; akkuṇṭikaikaḷ mikak kuṟukiya vāyuṭaiyavai; kallaip poḷintāṉṟa pōṉṟa tōṟṟattaṉavumām(?) iccēkkaikaḷaic cuṟṟilum naṟumpūpputarkaḷ pala vayaṅkuṅ kāṭci, maṉattāṉōkku vārkkup periyatōr iṟumpūtu payakkiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ uṟaiyum aṟiñarāya amaṇartām varuttamaṟap paṟittu vaḻipaṭutaṟkeṉavē ippūñceṭikaḷum piṟavum vaittu vaḷarkkap peṟukiṉṟavām.
iṉi, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟōṭu, kalvi, aṟivu, nalloḻukkam, aṟaṉaruḷuṭaimai mutaliyavai maṇakkum ciṟappuṭaimaiyāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkum aracavaiyiluṟaiyum periyōrāya kāvitimākkaḷ mutaliyōratu nalaṉum, piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟār.
kāvitimākkaḷāvār kāvitiyeṉṉum paṭṭattai aracaṉāl tarappeṟṟavarāvar. avarkaḷuḷ īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭupavar, aracaṉiṭattu appaṭṭattaippeṟṟu, amaiccukkaṭam pūṇṭoḻukum amāt tiyarāvar. ivarkaḷ muṟai vēṇṭiṉārkkum, kuṟaivēṇṭi ṉārkkum accam, avalam, ārvam mutaliya nikaḻāmaṟpōkki, ceṟṟamātal uvakaiyātal oṉṟēṉum oru tiṟattēṉum ceyyātu, tammaiyum kāttu, ñemaṉkōl (tulākkōl) pōlum cemmaittāya naṭunilai piṟaḻāta koḷkai mēṟkoṇṭava rāvar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ aṟaṅ kūṟavaiyattārōṭu uṭaṉiruntu, aracaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻum naṉṟum tītumkaṇṭu, attīṅkukaḷaiyā rāyntu avaṟṟuḷ avaṉ mīṭṭum oḻukāvāṟu aṭakki, aṉpum aṟaṉum avaṉaiviṭṭu oḻiyā vakaiyiṟkāttu oḻukum pērāṇmai talaiciṟantavar. ittoḻilkaḷil īṭupaṭuṅkāl, ivarkaḷ tammaiyum paḻipāvaṅkaḷ vantaṇukāvāṟu nīkkikkōṭalum, paranta pukaḻai yipparantavulakil niṟuttalum ceytu koḷḷum tiṟalmikkavar. itupaṟṟiyē ivarkaḷai āṉṟavintaṭaṅkiya koḷkai mēmpaṭṭa cāṉṟōrkaḷōṭu oppavaittu eṇṇupa. ēṉeṉiṉ, iccāṉṟōrum ariya vēḷvikaḷ palaceytu māvicumpu (tēvarulaku) vaḻaṅkum vālaṟivum, vēḷvikkālattup paranta tammārpiṭaip pūcappeṟum naṟiya cantaṉappūccu muṭaiyavarē. ivarkaḷ tam aracaṉaic cūḻavirukkuṅkāl, uyariya paṭṭāṭai yuṭuttu, muṭiyil mayirkkaṭṭaṇintu (talaippākaiyaṇintu) ciṟanta tōṟṟaṅ koṇṭu tikaḻvar.
vāṇipanalaṉ:-
itukāṟum, maturaikkāñciyiṉ karupporuḷum, maturai yāṇṭa maṉṉaratu āṭci nalaṉum, maturaiyiṉ vaḷattu oru pakutiyum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. iṉi maturaiyiṉ vāṇipa nalaṉ, viḻavu nalaṉ ākiya ēṉainalaṉkaḷaiyum, kūṟi ikkāñciyiṉ coṟ poruṇalaṅkaḷaip piṟanūlkaḷōṭu oppu nōkkumiṭattut tōṉṟum cila nūṇ poruḷkaḷum, avvārāycci yiṭaip pōtarum akkāla oḻukka nalaṉkaḷaiyum kūṟi ikkaṭṭuraiyai muṭikkiṉṟēṉ.
vaṭitta coṟkaḷāṟṟoṭukkap peṟṟu, āyuntoṟum puttiṉpam payakkum polivu kūrntu, kaṭavuḷiṉpattaik kaṟpārk kūṭṭum iṉpa nilaiyamāya ippāṭṭiṉ kaṇvarum vaṇikar pala tiṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru cilar paṇṭam pakarunar āvar. ivarkaḷ malai, nilam, nīr mutaliya viṭaṅkaḷiṟ paṭuvaṉavum, piṟaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟupāṉmai kiṭaikkap peṟuvaṉavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu palavāy vēṟupaṭṭu aḻaku ciṟakkum maṇi, muttu, poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut tāmpiṟatēyaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu vantuḷḷa arum paṇṭaṅkaḷai māṟi vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ koḷvatūum mikai paṭātu; koṭuppatūum kuṟaipaṭātu. mēlum, ivarkaḷ, tamakku ōtiya illaṟaneṟiyiṟ ṟavaṟātu niṉṟu, aṟattāṟu pōṟṟi āruyirppēṇum iyalpiṉarāvar. ivar tam illaṅkaḷ, tiraṇṭa kuṉṟaṅkaḷ pala mika aṇmaiyavāy niṟpa avaṟṟaik kāṇ pārkkup pulaṉākum cīriya tōṟṟattaiyum, viṇṇiṭaip paṟantu tiriyum paruntukaḷ iruntu iḷaippāṟip piṉṉar uyarap paṟakkum uyarcciyaiyum, palavēṟu ciṟpattoḻil viḷaṅkum māṇpaiyum uṭaiya nalla illaṅkaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ palvakaip paṇṭamoṭu uṇavum mikkuṟṟup poliyum.
niṟka, kōṭupōḻ kaṭainar mutal kammiyar īṟākavuḷḷa vaṇikar palar uḷar. avarkaḷuṭkōṭupōḻkaṭainar caṅku kaḷaiyaṟuttu vaḷai mutaliyaṉavākacceytu viṟpar; tirumaṇi kuyiṟṟuṉar eṉpavar aḻakiya maṇikaḷai vāṅkittuḷaiyiṭṭu mālai mutaliyaṉavākat toṭuttuviṟpar; iḻaipuṉainar poṉṉaik kāyccit tūymaiceytu oḷḷiya pūṇāraṅkaḷ ceyvar; ivarkaḷ taṭṭār eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭuvar; poṉ vāṇikar varap peṟum poṟṟiraḷai uraiyiṭṭu nōkki māṟṟuk kāṇpavarāvar; kaliṅkam pakarnar uyariya paṭṭāṭaikaḷum puṭavaikaḷum viṟpar; cempu niṟaikoṇmār eṉpār cempiṉai niṟuttu vilaikku vāṅkum vaṇikarāvār; vampu niṟai muṭinar kaccukkaḷai yaḻaku peṟa muṭiyiṭṭut tamtoḻil nayamtōṉṟap puṉaintuviṟkum vaṇikar. ēṉaippūvāṇikar naṟiyapūkkaḷum pukaikamaḻum cāntum āyntu toṭuttu viṟṟal ceyvar; cittirakārikaḷ eṉpār makaḷirāvar; ivar kūriya matinuṭpa muṭaiyavar; eḻutappeṟum vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ naṉku viḷaṅkum vakaiyil, ivar tam kūriya vuṇarvumuṟṟum porunta eḻutiya ōviyaṅkaḷai, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oppak kāṭṭa, avvaḷaviṟ kāṇpār kaṭpulaṉellāṅ kavarntu niṟkum toḻiṟpēṟu ciṟakkum ciṟappiṉar. ivarkaṭku ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutiviṟṟalē toḻil. kammiyar eṉappaṭuvōr antikkaṭaiyil puṭavai viṟpavar. periyarum ciṟiyarumākat tiraṇṭu, nālvakait teruk kaḷilum, makkaḷ, oruvar kāloṭu oruvar kāluṟa neruṅki niṟpa, teṇṇīrttiraiyāl maṭippup peṟum yāṟṟaṟal oppa, periyavum ciṟiyavumāya pala maṭippuṭaivaikaḷai virittu vaittup piṟarkku viṟpar.
cōṟutarunar: ivarkaḷ varuvārkkuc cōṟiṭutaṟkeṉat taṉippaṭac cōṟiṭucālaikaḷamaittu, aṅkup pāṟcōṟum pālum koṇarn taḷippar. varupavar tamittuvariṉum, kūṭṭamāy variṉum cōṟiṭutalil varaiviṉṟi yaḷippar. aṅku accōṟ ṟoṭu tēṉ, palaviṉcuḷai, māṅkaṉi, pākaṟkāy, vāḻaikkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy mutaliyaṉavum, vāḻaippaḻam, muntiri kaippaḻam mutaliya paḻavakai kaḷum, ilaikkaṟikaḷ (kīraivakai), kaṟkaṇṭu, kiḻaṅku mutaliyaṉavum ceṟintu kiṭakkum. ivaṟṟoṭukalantu maṇakkum ūṉumpiṟavum uraippār uraiyiṉaiyum pāṭuvār pāṭṭiṉaiyum ikantu tikaḻum.
celvarkaḷ: ivarkaḷ cekkar vāṉattai yoppac civantu nuṇaṅkum oḷḷiya āṭaiyuṭuttu, araiyiṟ poṉ puṉainta uṭaivāḷaip polivupeṟakkaṭṭi, tōḷil mēlāṭai kiṭantacaiya, piṟakkiṭāp perumaiyuṟṟa tam tiruntaṭiyiṟ kaccam tiṉṟa kaḻaliruntu olippa, vēppanteriyalum, ceṅkaḻunīr mālaiyum aṇikiḷarum mārpiṟṟikaḻum ārattōṭu kalantu viḷaṅka, kāṟṟiṉuṅ kaṭiya kataḻpari pūṭṭiya tiṇṇiya tērmī tivarntu, nāṟpuṟamum kālōr (kālāṭkaḷ) kāval ceyya viraintu cellum mēmpāṭuṭaiyavar. varuvārkku varaiyātu koṭukkum vāṉampōlum vaṇmaiyiṉarākiya ivarkku, avvaḷḷaṉmaikkēṟpa vaḷaṅkeḻumiya celvam ciṟantu pukaḻ vaḷarkkum.
iṅṅaṉam, vīramum, veṟṟiyum, vaṇmaiyum, celvamu moruṅkē vāykkappeṟṟa ivarkku, uḷattē kaṭavuḷaṉpu perukkeṭuttuc cellutal nammaṉōrāl maṟakkaṟpāṟṟaṉṟu. ivarkaḷ anti viḻaveṭuttalum, ataṉai yeṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu, puṇṇiya nīrāṭi, mācaṟa viḷaṅkum yākkaiyarāy, naṟiya maṇan taruvaṉavaṟṟaip peritu aṇintu, avvaṇitalā leḻum maṇam kamaḻ nāṟṟam teruvuṭaṉ kamaḻac cellutalum niṉaittoṟum pēriṉpam payappaṉa. niṟka, ivarkaḷ eṭukkum viḻāviṉaik kāṇum makaḷir nirainilai māṭaṅkaḷiṉ aramiyantōṟum niṉṟum, tāmaṇintuḷḷa poṟpūṇkaḷoṭu cīṟaṭic cilampu pulampa iḻintum viḻākkāṇṭalum, atukālai avar tantōṟṟam vāṉara makaḷiratoppa, āynta poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa iḻaikaḷuntoṭikaḷum pēraḻaku ceytalum, akakkāṭciyāl  (Imagination) nōkkuvārkkut tōṉṟātu oḻiyā. piṉṉar, nāyaṉmārkaḷ eḻuntaruḷuṅkāl eṭukkappaṭum ‘tiṇkāḻuṟṟa viyaliruvilōtam” (periya akaṉṟa koṭikaḷ) kāṟṟacaiviṉāl teṇkaṭaṟ ṟiraipōlap puṭaippa, ammakaḷiratu oṇkuḻai tikaḻum oḷikeḻu tirumukam, maḻaikkaṇ maṟaiyum matiya moppat tōṉṟupu maṟaiyum. iṉi, ic celvarkaḷ, maḻuvāḷ neṭi yōṉai mutalvaṉākakkoṇṭu ēṉait teyvavoḷiyum, vāṭāta pūkkaḷum, imaiyāta nāṭṭamum, nāṟṟavuṇavu (avi yuṇavu) ṅkoṇṭa vurukeḻu periyōrāya māyōṉ, murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaṭku uyarpali koṭuttu avvanti viḻaviṉai muṭippar. akkālai, eḻum tūriyaṅkaḷiṉ oli alai kaṭalaiyu maṭakkum.
koṇṭi makaḷir (parattaiyar):-
ivar *vāṉara makaḷiraiyotta vaṉappuṭaiyavar. mēlum ivar nīṉiṟa vicumpiṉkaṇ amarntu viḷaiyāṭṭayarum am makaḷiraiyoppavē, tammaik kāṇpavar maṉattai varutti, avaratu poruḷaikkavarum parattaiyarāvar. ivarkaḷ oṇcuṭar parappum viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉ nalloḷiyil, palarākac cēraneruṅki, āḻnta nīr niṟainta tuṟaikaḷiṉmuṉ parantu kiṭakkum nuṇ maṇaliṟ kūṭiyāṭuvar. akkālai, mutalyāmattil vācittaṟkuriya yāḻicai yoru maruṅku eḻa, avvicaik kēṟpa, ivarkaḷ tāḻntum, arukē olikkum muḻavoliyoṭu ottum maṉamakiḻn tāṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam maṇal viḷaiyāṭṭu muṭintatum, avviṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, kuvaḷaimalar, nīrkkīḻc cevvarumpu mutali yavaṟṟāṟ ṟoṭukkappeṟṟa mālaittoṭarpala, tam āṭaiyiṉ vaṭimpil viḻumvaṇṇa muṭuttu, nīr tiraṇṭāṟpōṉṟa veṇpū mālaikaḷaik koṇṭaiyiṟ cūṭic cēyarum aṇiyarumākap palarāyt tam nalaṉ nayantu varum pala iḷamaiyuṅ celvamu mikkārai, vañcaṉaiyum poyyuṅ kalanta coṟkaḷāl, mutaliṟ kūṭṭik koṇṭu, avaratu nuṇpūṇākam tam ākattil vaṭuk koḷḷum vakaiyil muyaṅkit tam poyyiṉpattai yavarkku nalkuvar. aṅṅaṉam, aṉpuṭaiyār pōl muyaṅkiya muyakkattai avar poruḷ tarumaḷavu maṟaittalālum, karuttu muṟṟum nayantuvantavaruṭaiya celvattaik kavarum muṟaiyilē yīṭu paṭṭiruttalālum ivarkaḷ tarum iṉpam poyyiṉpam eṉap paṭukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ celvam muṟṟumkeṭat tāmvāṅki, nuṇtātu uṇṭu piṉṉar at tātu niṟaintuḷḷa pūviṉait tuṟantu cellum meṉciṟai vaṇṭiṉam oppa, avar kūṭṭattai, (tammaip puṇarntu, tam peruñ celvattai yiḻantōratu kūṭṭattai), avar neñcam ēmāntu kalaṅka, immakaḷir nērē kaiviṭṭuppirintu celvar. celpavar mīṇṭum, tam polivu kulaintukiṭakkum paṇikaḷaiyum oppaṉaikaḷaiyum perumaiyum aḻakum naṉkamaiyap puṉaintu, miku nala meytiṉa varāyc ceṅkaḻunīr malarkaḷil curumpupaṭuvaṉavum, ēṉaippūkkaḷum koṇṭu, maḻaikku malarnta malar neruṅkiya oru ciṟu tūṟoppat tam kuḻaliṟ cūṭi, pōtaviḻum apputu malarkaḷiṉ naṟumaṇam teruveṅkum kamaḻa, vaḷaikuluṅkak kaivīci, maṇaṅkamaḻ maṉai tōṟum paḻantēr vāḻkkaip paṟavaipōṟ ceṉṟu, poruḷ tarutaṟkuriya iḷaiyōr palaruṭaṉ viḷaiyāṭṭayarvar.
pāciḻaimakaḷir: ivarkaḷ kuṭippeṇkaḷāvar. ivar kaḷuṭaiya illaṅkaḷ aṇaṅkuṭaiya nallillaṅkaḷākum. ivarkaḷ, koḻuṅkuṭic celvarum piṟarum mēya tam illat tiruntu, maṇam puṇarntōṅkiya paciya iḻaiyum oṭṭaṟṟa poṉvaḷai yalum aṇintu, irākkālaṅkaḷil, oṇ cuṭar viḷakkaṅkaḷiṉ nalloḷiyilamarntu, icaiyēḻum vāytta cīriya ciṟiya yāḻeṭuttu, paṇmuṟaiyil māṟiyicaittut tammai nayakkum āruyirk kātalarkkuc ceviccuvaiyamutam ciṟakkavūṭṭit taḻuvi, tam uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇaiyun tammiṭattē taṭuttuk koḷḷun takuti mēmpaṭṭavarāvar.
vaḷamaṉaimakaḷir: ivar peruṅkuṭip piṟantu perumaiyum, celvañ ceḻikkum ciṟappuṅkoṇṭa maṉaimakaḷirāvar. ivaruṭ cilar “irumai yiṉpamum payakkum aṟivaṟinta makkaḷaip peṟṟēm” eṉat tam kaṇavaruppa, makkaḷaippayantu, melivu mēvum puṉiṟum tīrntu, iṟaivaṉaruḷāl ōr iṭukkaṇum aṟṟukkuḷattu nīrāṭṭayarvar. atukaṇṭu, talaiccūl koṇṭa makaḷir, tāmum iṅṅaṉ oru iṭukkaṇumiṉṟi makkaḷaip peṟutal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṭavuḷaipparavik kuṟaitīrntapiṉ, polinta cuṟṟattārōṭu, vaḻipāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭappaṭum poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ, peruntōḷ koṇṭa cāliṉi eṉṉum tēva rāṭṭiyōṭu, tivaviṉai yāḻiṉ taṇṭil niṟuttic cevvaḻip paṇṇicaittu, kural eṉappaṭum narampu kūṭum vaṉṉattāl nalamparappi, appaṇṇukkum vaṉṉattukkum oppa muḻavaṅkaḷ olippa, melliya ciṟupaṟai (ākuḷi) kaṟaṅka, makaḷirpalar viḷak kēnti muṉcella, naṉmāmayileṉa meṉ mela naṭantu ceṉṟu cūḻvantu, kaiyāṟṟoḻutu vaṇaṅkiya piṟaku, tām paṭaitta pāṟ cōṟu mutaliya maṭaipporuḷkaḷai yuṇpar.
ūrkāppāḷar: irākkālaṅkaḷil mūṉṟām yāmattil ūraiccuṟṟit tiritarum kāvalarāvar. piṭiyāṉaiyiṉ tōl pōlum iyaṟkaik karuniṟam vāynta iruṇiṟak karuñ caṭṭaiyum, marantuṇippiṉum, kal uṭaippiṉum vāymaṭiyāk kūrmai koṇṭa nilamakaḻum uḷiyum, tūkkiṭṭavāḷum, toṭu tōṟ kōtta ceruppumē ivarkaḷ kaiyakattiruppaṉa. ivarkaḷ kaṇ māṟāṭavar (viḻittakaṇ imaikku maḷavilē maṟaikiṉṟa kaḷvar) oṭuṅkum otukkiṭaṅkaḷai nuṉittu, piṟar teriyātapaṭi āṇṭu maṟaintu niṉṟu, vayakkaḷiṟṟiṉ varavu pārkkum vayappuli pōla, virainta vuḷḷamoṭu nōkkum tuñcāk kaṇṇum, pēy mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum añcākkoḷkaiyum uṭaiyar. aṉṟiyum, ivarkaḷ, kaḷavuttoḻil vallārāṟ ciṟappittukkūṟappaṭum toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷai yeḷitil aṟintāḷum āṇmaiyum, nuṇmaiceṟinta ariya nūlkaḷ palavum ārāyum nuṇṇaṟivum paṭaittavar. ivar kaṭpulaṉāki, piṉṉart tappak karuti muyal vārkku orukālum tapputal iyalātu. ivarkaḷ ūkkaruṅkaṇai yiṉarātaliṉ, atu kūṭātu pōkiṉṟatu. niṟka, ivarkaḷuṭaiya toḻil nayappō (Love towards the discharge of duties) yāvarum mikap pōṟṟaṟpālatām. tēr vaḻaṅku teruvil nīr tiraṇ ṭoḻukumāṟu, perumaḻai peyyum naṭunāḷ yāmattum, ivarkaḷ, oru ciṟitum acaivu ilarāki niṉṟu, tam toḻilai nayam vantu ceytoḻukukiṉṟavar. iṉi, ippeṟṟiyarāya kāvalar irunta nāḷil kaḷavut toḻil mēṟkoṇṭirunta kaṇmāṟāṭavar, iyalpu oruciṟitu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṭavar karuniṟamum nuṇmaiyuṅ koṇṭa cēlai yoṉṟai yaraiyiṟ kaṭṭi, ataṉkaṇ kūriya coṭṭai (uṭaivāḷ) kaḷaic ceruki, iru tuṭaikaṭkum iṭaiyiṟceluttip puṟattē teriyāmal maṟaittuk koḷvar. piṉṉar pala niṟaṅkaḷamaittup polivu ceyyappeṟṟa nīlakkacciṉaiyaṇintu, ataṉ kīḻmelliya nūlēṇi yoṉṟaic cuṟṟik koṇṭumiruppar. ivarkaḷiṉ nacai muṟṟum celvarkaḷ vaittuḷḷa pēraṇikalaṅkaḷiṉ mēlatākavē niṟkum. (ivaratu toḻiṟṟiṟam mutaliyavaṟṟaip poruḷārāyc ciyiṟ kūṟukiṉṟēṉ.)
aracaṉāl iṉpanukarap peṟutaṟkuriya mātar: aracaṉ taṉ aracirukkai nakarākiya maturaiyiṉ kaṇṇiruntu ivarkaḷ poṟ kalaṅkaḷil tarum iṉpakkuṭiyiṉai yuṇṭu tuyilkoḷḷap pukuvatu marapu. kāmapāṉameṉappaṭuvatu
“paḻumiḷa kukka pāṟai neṭuñcuṉai
 muḻumutaṟ kokkiṉ tīṅkaṉi yutirnteṉa
 puḷḷeṟi piracamoṭu īṇṭip palaviṉ
 nekiḻntuku naṟumpaḻam viḷainta tēṟal”
pōlum oruvakait tēṟal. itu naṟumpūkkaḷiliruntu peṟap paṭutaliṉ naṟumaṇamum iṉcuvaiyum mikkuḷatu. itaṉai yuṇṭa piṉṉarē maṉṉaṉ paḷḷikkuc cēṟalum tuyil kōṭalum nikaḻum. avarkkup pōkamakaḷirākum ivarkaḷatu vaṉappu īṇṭuk kūṟap paṭukiṟatu.
nāṟṟicaiyum tam ciṉaikaḷ paṭarntu centīppōlum pūkkaṇ malarntu curumpu murala, aḻakumikku viḷaṅkum acōkam poḻiloṉṟil, pakal muṟṟum cuṭar poḻintu kuṭavaraiyēṟiya viḷaṅkukatir ñāyiṟu taṉ iḷaveyil parappuṅkāṟṟōṉṟum vaṇṇamum vaṉappum poruntiya mēṉiyuṭaiyarāy, naṭuvē maṇikaḷ vaittiḻaitta tāvil poṟkalaṉkaḷ aṇintu viḷaṅkum immakaḷir, tampeṟalaruṅ kaṟpiṉāl, ulakamuṟṟum viḷakka muṟumpaṭi mēmpāṭu peṟṟavarāvar. ivarkaḷ mayil pōlum cāyalum māviṉ taḷirpōlum mēṉiyum, attaḷiriṉ puṟattirukkum īrkkuppōlum titalaiyumuṭaiyavar. ivar tam paṟkaḷ oṇmaiyum kūrmaiyum peṟṟavai. vaḷaviya kātukaḷ oḷḷiyamakākkuḻai yaṇiyappaṭṭut tāḻntu viḷaṅkum. mēlum, ivarkaḷ kuḷaṅkaḷil neruṅkiyiruppatum, neruppup pōlum itaḻkaḷai yuṭaiyatāyt teyvaṅkaṭku uriyatumākavuḷḷa tāmaraiyiṉ tātupaṭum periya pūppōlum vāṇmukattiṉarāyk kaikaḷil āytoṭi pūṇṭu viḷaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷōṭu kōtai yāṟ polinta cēkkaik kaṇ maṉṉaṉ iṉpanukarntu iṉṟuyil koḷvaṉ.
mālaikālacciṟappu:
neṭunakarāya maturaiyiṉ ellaiyellām, pakal muṟṟum oḷiyum veppamum oruṅkukoṉṟu, taṉ ciṉamāṟiyateṉum vakai, ñāyiṟu taṉ oḷiyaic cērakkoṇṭukuṭamutaṟ kuṉṟam cēra, kuṇamutal varaiyil muḻumatiyantōṉṟit taṉ nilavu virittup pakaleṉumpaṭi iravuc ceyyum. peṇmaic ciṟappum māṉpiṇaipōlum nōkkamumuṭaiya makaḷir, neṭuñcuṭar parappum viḷakkukkaḷaiyēṟṟi, palavēṟu vakaippaṭṭa aṇi kaḷaip puṉaintu, taṇṇaṟuṅ kaḻunīr malarai mālaiyākat toṭut taṇintu, tām tam kūntaliṟ pūciya naṟuviya mayirc cantaṉattai alaittu nīkkik kattūri, cantaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟai yaraittu oṉṟākak kalantu pūci, meṉṉūl āṭaikku naṟumpukai yūṭṭiyuṭuttu viḷaṅkuvar. nayanta kātalaraip pirinta makaḷirō varuntutal ceyvar.
iravucciṟappu: mutalyāmam:
ivyāmattiṉkaṇ, mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa koṇṭi makaḷir poruḷ tarutaṟkuriya iḷaiñarōṭu viḷaiyāṭi, avar maṉattaik karaittut tamvayappaṭutta muyaluvar. pāciḻaimakaḷir tam kaṇavarkku yāḻicaiyum muyakka viṉpattaiyuntantu, tam uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇaik kāttukkoḷḷuvar. māṟupāṭumikkup porum perum pōrkaḷil, niṉṟa aṭi māṟāmaiyāṟpaṭum vaṭuppaṭṭa neṟṟiyum, pōrē virumpum uḷḷamum, curumpu muralum tumpaippūc cūṭutalumuṭaiya maṟavar, (vīrarkaḷ) tōṭṭiyāl vaṭuppaṭṭa nutalum, pōrtāṅkum vaṉmaiyum, periya kaiyiṉaiyuṅ koṇṭa peruṅ kaḷiṟukaḷaikkoṇarntu, kolaipaḻakku mukattāl, palakālum eṭuttu ōṭṭa, akkālai, ak kaḷiṟukaḷ peruvicaikoṇṭu eḻutalum, avaṟṟiṉ vicaiyaik kāṇṭaṟ poruṭṭu, immaṟavarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ muṉpu ōṭippārppār. avai tammaip piṭittukkoṇṭu tammēl vārāmal añci mīḷutaṟkeṉa, avaṟṟiṉ kālkaḷiṟ potukkumpaṭi, cila pala  kappaṇaṅkaḷai, *avai varumvaḻiyil nilattē citaṟuvar. ip payiṟci muṭintatum, ivarkaḷ kaṭṭeḷivaiyuṇṭu makiḻ ciṟantu terukkaḷiṟ ṟiritaruvar. vaḷamaṉaimakaḷir putalvarp payantu puṉiṟṟup melivuntīrntu kuḷanīrāṭalum, avaraik kāṇum talaic cūl makaḷir kaṭavuḷaip paravutalum ceyvar. orutti nāṟṟam, tōṟṟam mutaliyavaikaḷāl vēṟupaṭṭāḷāka, avaṭku av vēṟupāṭṭukkaṇ, murukaṉāl vantatu eṉa veṟiyāṭṭayarum vēlaṉ avaṭaṉ peṟṟōrkku nayampaṭakkūṟi, avarait taṉ collāl vaḷaittukkoṇṭu, calli, karaṭi mutaliya iṉṉiyaṅkaḷ kaṟaṅka, kārkālattu malarum iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya kuṟiñci malaraic cūṭi, kaṭampucūṭi aḻaku mikum murukaṉait taṉ meyk kaṇṇiṟutti, vaḻipāṭu ceyvaṉ. atupōtu mātarmakaḷirum matavali maintarum tātupōlum erupparanta maṉṟaṅkaḷiṟ kuravaiyayara, ēṉaiyōr uraiyum pāṭṭum iṭaiyiṭaiyē viravappāṭiyum āṭiyum makiḻvar. iṅṅaṉam, pala nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻvataṉāl pala vēṟuvakaippaṭṭa kampalai mikuntu, pēricai koṇṭa naṉṉaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal koṇṭāṭum piṟanta nāṭkoṇṭāṭṭattaṉṟu eḻum ōcaiyai ottu imiḻum.
pāṉāṭkoṇṭakaṅ kulyāmam: (iraṇṭām yāmam)
itu patiṉaintām nāḻikaiyai muṭivākakkoṇṭa pakuti yākum. itaṉ kaṇ, caṅkukaḷai muḻakkum muḻakkam oru puṭaiyaṭaṅ kiyaviya, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vilaikūṟi viṟpaṉai ceyyum vaṇikar tam kaṭaikaḷiṉ caṭṭakkāl eṉappaṭum kāḻaic cāyttut tam neṭiya kaṭaikaḷai yaṭaittukkoṇṭu illam ēkuvar. maṭappam matarppu mutaliya uyariya kuṇaṅkaḷum oḷḷiya iḻaikaḷum koṇṭamakaḷir paḷḷiyiṟ kaṇṭuñcal ceyvar. variporuntiya tēṉiṟāloppa, aṭai, mōtakam, karaittamāvappam mutaliya vaṟṟai viṟkum  appavāṇikar avaṟṟōṭē tūṅkuvaṉarāy uṟaṅka, kūttāṭal payiṉṟa vayiriyarum piṟarum kūttāṭ ṭoḻintu uṟaṅka, pāṭu avintu aṭaṅkiya paṉikkaṭalpōl pāyil kaṇvaḷarvōr iṉitu kaṇṇayarvar.

24. naṉṉaṉum paraṇarum
naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kālattukkuc ciṟitu piṟkālattil cēraṉāṭṭu oru pakutiyil iruntu aracu purinta kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ. ivaṉ vēḷir kulattil tōṉṟiyavaṉ; ivaṉuṭaiya peṟṟōr varalāṟu oṉṟum terintilatu.
ivaṉuṭaiya nāṭu koṇkāṉa nāṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ituvē piṉṉar, koṅkaṇanāṭeṉṟum, koṅku nāṭeṉṟum maruvi vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṟṟeṉṟu kūṟupa. itaṉuṭaiya parappu, ikkālamuṟaippaṭi, maicūr nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyum, cēlam kōyamputtūr ākiya nāṭukaḷum, nīlakiri mēlakiri mutaliya kiḻakkumalait toṭariṉ pakutiyum koṇṭiruntatu. innāṭṭiṉ kaṇ pāram, pirampu, viyalūr, pāḻi eṉṉum ciṟanta nakaraṅkaḷum, pāḻi, ēḻil mutaliya malaikaḷum iruntaṉa. in nāṭṭiṉkaṇ koṇkāṉam eṉṉum ciṟanta malai yiruntamaiyiṉ, ataṉaic cūḻnta innāṭum koṇkāṉa nāṭu eṉap paṭalāyiṟṟu. in nāṭṭiṉkaṇ poṉmikutiyākak kiṭaikkum.
mēṟkūṟiya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ pāram eṉpatu ivaṉukkē urit tāyiṉum nāṭṭiṉ parappuṭaimaiyum, āṭciyarumai yuṭaimaiyum kuṟittu, miñili eṉṉum vīraṉ oruvaṉ āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, iruntatu. pāḻi eṉṉum nakaram, pāḻi yeṉṉum malaiyaṭiyilirunta pērūr; itaṉkaṇ, naṉṉaṉ tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷai vēḷir palar kāvalceyya vaittiruntāṉ. itu tēṉvaḷam ciṟantatu.
naṉṉaṉ, palaraip puṟaṅ kaṇṭu avar aruṅkalam koṇarntu pāḻikkaṇ tokuttu vaittamaiyē yaṉṟi, "iṭaṉiṉṟi iran tōrkku illeṉṟal iḻi" veṉak karuti varaiyātu vīcum vaḷḷaṉmaiyum, pulavar pāṭum pukaḻum paṭaittavaṉ. ivaṉ kālattiṉ "nāṇmaṅkalam" eṉṉum piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam nikaḻntatu.
ivaṉ orukāl koṇṭa vañciṉam, toḻilāl muṟṟup peṟuṅkāṟum, atu taṇiyāṉ eṉap peṟappaṭuvatu avaṉ pōrc ceyalkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ivaṉ kālattil piṇṭaṉ, atikaṉ eṉpār mika meyvali paṭaittiruntaṉar. ivaruḷ  atikaṉ eṉpavaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ āṇaikkīḻ niṟkum kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ. ākavē, avaṉ atikaṉpacumpūṇpāṇṭiyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ. ivaṉ pōrttoḻilil mikka vaṉmai yuṭaiyavaṉ; nallicai tarum iravalarkkup perum poruḷ aḷittu aḻiyāp pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ; villēntiya paṭaivīrar palar koṇṭavaṉ. naṉṉaṉ iv viruvaraiyum veṉṟu perum poruḷ koṇṭatōṭamaiyātu, apporuḷkaḷai avarnāṭṭu yāṉai mītēṟṟi, avartam makaḷir kūntalāl kayiṟu tirittup piṇittuk koṇarntu taṉatu pāḻinakariṉ kaṇ vēḷir kāppa vaittaṉaṉ.
naṉṉaṉ ceṉṟa viṭaṅkaḷilellām taṉ veṉṟiyē viḷaṅkac ceytaṟkut tuṇaiyāka ēṟṟai, atti, kaṅkaṉ, kaṭṭi, puṉṟuṟai, kaṇaiyaṉ eṉap pala vīrar iruntaṉar. ivaruḷ kaṭṭi yeṉpāṉ ciṟanta vīraṉallaṉ; ivaṉ tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉpā ṉōṭu pōrceyyat toṭaṅkic ceṉṟavaṉ, avaṉuṭaiya avaikkaḷattu eḻum kiṇaiyōcai kēṭṭu añci yōṭiṉāṉ. orukāl, naṉṉaṉ, ivartam tuṇaiyuṭaṉ, perumpūṇ ceṉṉi yōṭu pōrceyyac ceṉṟu, avaṉuṭaiya paṭaivīraṉākiya paḻaiyaṉaik kolla, ataṉāṟ ciṉam ciṟantu perum pōruṭaṟṟiya ceṉṉikkup puṟantantu ōṭiṉaṉ.
naṉṉaṉ paṭaivīraruḷ miñili yeṉpavaṉ ciṟanta vīraṉ; naṉṉaṉ pukaḻkku mutaṟkāraṇa mākiyōṉ im miñiliyē eṉak kōṭal vēṇṭum. ivaṉ aṉṉi yeṉṉum vīraṉukku makaṉ. inta aṉṉi, akkālattil perum valic ciṟappuṭaiyaṉā yirunta titiyaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭaiya kāvalmarattait taṭintu, vēḷevvi eṉpavaṉ kūṟiya nalluraiyaiyum kēḷātu, avaṉ ciṉattiṟ kāḷākip piṉ avaṉōṭu pōr ceytu, kuṟukkai yeṉṉu miṭattup puṟantantu vīḻntaṉaṉ. piṉṉar avvaṉṉi, kōcar kaḷōṭu porutu tōlviyuṟa avar avaṉ kaṇkaḷaikkaḷaintu aḻittaṉar. icceytiyāl āṟāc ciṉamuṟ ṟirunta miñiliyai naṉṉaṉ taṉ paṭaittalaivaṉākak koṇṭu, akkōcarkaḷai veṉṟaḻittu, vākaicūṭiṉaṉ, titiyaṉ iṟantapiṉ, miñili avaṉuṭaiya aḻuntūriṉ kaṇ ciṟappuṭaṉ ulāviṉaṉ.
akkālattil, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭiccēral eṉṉum cēravēntaṉukku āyeyiṉaṉ eṉṉum paṭait talaivaṉ oru vaṉiruntaṉaṉ. iccēraṉukkum, naṉṉaṉukkum ekkāraṇattālō pakaimaiyuṇṭāka, eyiṉaṉ miñiliyōṭu pōr ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. eyiṉaṉukkup palar miñiliyiṉ valiyuṭaimai viḷaṅkak kūṟiyum eyiṉaṉ koḷḷātu pōr ceytu oḻintāṉ. itaṉā luṇṭāṉa ciṉam nār muṭic cēralukku viraivil aṭaṅkiṟṟaṉṟu; cēraṉukkum naṉṉaṉukkum kaṭampiṉ peru vāyil, vākaip peruntuṟai eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil pōr nikaḻntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ. vākaip perun tuṟaip pōrkkaṇṇē, naṉṉaṉ perumpōr ceytu taṉṉāṭiḻantu āviyu miḻantāṉ.
itu nikaḻnta ciṟitu kālattukku muṉṉar, peṇkolai oṉṟu purintu tīrāp paḻiyum peṟṟāṉ. kuṇam kuṟṟam iraṇṭiṉuḷ oṉṟē yuṭaiyār ulakat tilaraṉṟō! peṇṇorutti nīrāṭac ceṉṟavaḷ, nīrkkaṇ mitantu pōnta ivaṉukkuriya muṟṟāta paḻam oṉṟait tiṉṟoḻin tāḷāka. atu poṟāta ivaṉ, avaḷ tantai eṇpattoru kaḷiṟukaḷaiyum, avaḷ niṟai poṉcey pāvai koṭuppavum koḷḷātu, avaḷaik kolaiyē purintaḻintāṉ. ivaṉ orukāl oḷavaiyār taṉṉaip pāṭi vantamai yaṟintu avar varicaiyaṟintu paricil nalkā toḻiya, avar ivaṉai veṟuttup pāṭiṉar.
iṉi, ivaṉatu varalāṟu aṟitaṟkup pērutaviyā yirunta āciriyar paraṇar pāṭalkaḷai ovvoṉṟāka ārāyvām. ivaṟṟuḷ akanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷaṉavaṟṟuḷ palavaṟṟiṟku urai yiṉmaiyiṉ, uraiyum īṇṭuk kūṟukiṉṟōm. piḻai paṭuvaṉa vaṟṟait terikkumāṟu periyōrkaḷai vēṇṭukiṉṟēm.
āciriyar paraṇar akanāṉūṟṟil tām pāṭiya muppattu nāṉkupāṭṭukkaḷuḷ naṉṉaṉ varalāṟṟuk kiyaiyap patiṉāṉku pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ, ivaṉ tittaṉ, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōrai veṉṟa ceytiyum, āay pirampu eṉṉum pērūr ivaṉukkē uritteṉpatum piṟavumākiya ceytikaḷōṭu perum pālaṉavaṟṟuḷ miñiliyiṉ pōrcceyaliṉ nalaṅkaḷaiyum viḷakkic celkiṉṟār. ēṉaik kuṟuntokai, naṟṟiṇai mutaliya tokai nūlkaḷuḷ, naṉṉaṉ kōcaraiveṉṟamaiyum, peṇkolai purintamaiyum, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōrai veṉṟu avartam poruḷ kaḷai makaḷir kūntalāṟ ṟiritta kayiṟṟāṟ kaṭṭik koṇarntamaiyum piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟār.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ, tittaṉ, piṇṭaṉ mutaliyōrai veṉṟa ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu,
"neñcunaṭuṅ karumpaṭar tīra vantu
  kuṉṟuḻai naṇṇiya cīṟūr āṅkaṇ
  celīiya peyarvōḷ vaṇarcuri yaimpāl
  nuṇkōl akavunarp puranta pēricaic
  ciṉaṅkeḻu tāṉait tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ
  iraṅkunīrp parappiṟ kāṉalam peruntuṟait
  taṉantaru naṉkalam citaiyat tākkum
  ciṟuveḷ ḷiṟaviṉ kuppai yaṉṉa
 vuṟupakai tarūum moymmūcu piṇṭaṉ
 uṇmura ṇuṭaiyak kaṭanta veṉvēl
 icainal līkaik kaḷiṟuvīcu vaṇmakiḻp
 pārattut talaiva ṉāra naṉṉaṉ
 ēḻil neṭuvaraip pāḻic cilampiṟ
 kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷē
 valvil iḷaiyar perumakaṉ naḷḷi
 cōlai yaṭukkattuc curumpuṇa virinta
 kaṭavuṭ kānta ḷuḷḷum palavuṭaṉ
 iṟumpūtu kañaliya vāymalar nāṟi
 valliṉum vallā rāyiṉum ceṉṟōrkkuc
 cālaviḻ neṭuṅkuḻi niṟaiya vīcum
 māal yāṉai yāay kāṉattut
talaiyāṟṟu nilaiiya cēyuyar piṟaṅkal
vēyamaik kaṇṇiṭai puraiic
cēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē"	- eṉpatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: (kūṭṭam peṟāmaiyāl) neñcam naṭuṅkat takka poṟuttaṟkariya varuttam nīṅka vantu, kūṭṭattāl an nōyaip pōkkik, kuṉṟiṉuk kaṇittāṉa ciṟṟūriṉkaṭ celvāṉ peyarntu pōkiṉṟavaḷuṭaiya, neṟittuc curinta kūntaliṉaiyum, nuṇṇiya kōlaiyu muṭaiya pāṇmakaḷiraip pātukātta mikka pukaḻiṉaiyum, ciṉam poruntiya tāṉai yiṉaiyu muṭaiya tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṉuṭaiya olikkiṉṟa nīrp parappiṉaiyuṭaiya kāṉalam peruntuṟaikkaṇ poruḷ mikutiyākak koṇarum nalla marakkalaṅkaḷ citaiyumpaṭi mōtu kiṉṟa ciṟiya veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉkaḷiṉ tokutiyai yoppa, mikka pakaiyiṉaic ceppum valimai niṟainta piṇṭaṉ eṉpāṉait taṉ maṉamāṟupāṭu keṭumāṟu porutu veṉṟaveṟṟi poruntiya vēliṉaiyum, icaipayakkum varaiyā īkaiyum, kaḷiṟukaḷaip paricilarkkuk koṭukkum vaḷaviya makiḻcciyiiṉayumuṭaiya, "pāram" eṉṉum ūrukkut talaivaṉāṉa āram aṇinta, - naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭaiya "ēḻil" eṉṉum neṭiya malaiyaic cārnta "pāḻi" yeṉṉum malaiyiṉkaṭ kaḷittāṭum mayiliṉ kalāpattai yotta -tōḷ, valiya villiṉaiyuṭaiya vīrarkaḷuk kup periya talaivaṉāṉa naḷḷi yeṉpavaṉuṭaiya malaip pakkattut taḻaitta cōlaikkaṇ vaṇṭukaḷ matuvuṇṇa malarnta kaṭavuḷ virumpum kāntaḷ malarkaḷuḷḷum palavākap perumitam niṟainta melliya malarkaḷākiya kaiyiṉaittōṟṟuvittu, taṉṉai yuḷḷivantavar oṉṟu kūṟa vallarākavātal alla rākavātal iruppiṉum avarkku mikka cōṟṟiṉai avar tam neṭiya pāṉai niṟaiyumpaṭi aḷikkum periya yāṉaikaḷai yuṭaiya āay eṉpāṉatu kāṉattiṉ arukuḷḷa talaiyāṟu eṉṉumiṭattu nilaipeṟṟa mika vuyarnta malaiyiṉkaṇ uṇṭākiya mūṅkiliṉkaṇuviṉ iṭaip pakutiyai ottavāy, nammaip pirintu cēymaikkaṇṇē akaṉṟu nīṅkiṉa eṉiṉum, kūṭṭam peṟāmuṉ tanta naṭuṅkat takka tuyarattaiyē iṉiyum, tārā niṉṟaṉa vākalāṉ nām kūṭṭam peṟṟum pēṟēmāyiṉēm eṉpatu.
iḵtu, iravuk kuṟikkaṇ talaimakaḷaik kūṭi nīṅkum talaimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu. arumpaṭar tīra vantu cīṟūr āṅkaṇ, celīiya peyarvōḷ, kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷ āymalarnāṟi, vēyamaik kaṇṇiṭai purai iccēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅku tuyar tarum eṉa viyaiyum.
iravuk kuṟikkaṇ talaimakaḷait talaippeyvāṉ varupavaṉ kūṭṭam iṭaiyīṭu paṭuṅkolō eṉṉum eṇṇattaṉāy naṭuṅki varukiṉṟāṉākaliṉ, "neñcu naṭuṅku paṭar" eṉṟum appaṭartāṉum, maṟaippiṉum aṭaṅkātu mikutaliṉ, "arum paṭar" eṉṟum, kūṟiṉāṉ. āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum, ataṉai "iṟaippārkku, ūṟṟunīr pōla miku" meṉṟu kūṟiṉār. (kuṟaḷ 1161) uḻai, ēḻām vēṟṟumaipporuḷ paṭa vanta iṭaic col. kūṭṭam peṟṟavaḻi mayiṟkalāvam pōl meṉmaiyum uyarvu muṭaiyavāy iṉpam payanta ciṟappunōkkik "kaḷimayiṟ kalāvat taṉṉa tōḷ" eṉṟum, avviṉpam tantu peyarntavaḻit tārāmaiyāy "aṟitōṟaṟiyāmai kāṇpatu pōl maṟaintu tuyartarutaliṉ  "naṭuṅkutuyartarum" eṉṟum, neruṅkiyavaḻit taṉ piriviṉāluḷatākiya melivu kāṭṭiyum, nīṅkiyavaḻit taṉmaṉattē tōṉṟi niṉaippittum varuttutaliṉ "cēya vāyiṉum naṭuṅkutuyar tarumē" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. ummai, eccavummai.
pāṇmakaḷ kaiyil mūṅkiṟkōl uṭaiyaḷātal, taṉpiṟap puṇarttaṟkeṉpa. iṅṅaṉam pāṇmakaḷ orutti kātampari eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi nūliṉ kaṇṇum kūṟap paṭukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kaiyilum mūṅkiṟ kōloṉ ṟirunta teṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. veḷiyaṉ eṉpatu veḷiyam eṉṉum ūriṉaṉ eṉṉum poruḷa teṉpar. ivaṉ uṟantaikkaṇ iruntu aracāṇṭavaṉ eṉṟum, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉṟē āciriyar paraṇarē piṟitōriṭattum, "malitārt, tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ  uṟantai nāḷavai" (akam. 226) eṉak kūṟi yuḷḷār. ivaṉ "vīrai veḷiyaṉ tittaṉ" eṉṟum "vīrai vēṇmāṉ veḷiyaṉ tittaṉ" (naṟ. 58) eṉṟum "vīrai veḷiyaṉ tittaṉār" eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum cōḻamaṉṉaṉ. akanāṉūṟṟil "peruṅkaṭal mukanta iruṅkiḷaik koṇmū"eṉṟu toṭaṅkum pāṭṭu ivaṉ pāṭiyatu. veḷiyaṉallur eṉṉum ūr oṉṟu tiruvattipurattukkuk kiḻakkē mūṉṟumail tūrattil uḷatu.
kāṉaṟ peruntuṟaikkaṇ taṉam tarum valiya marakkalaṅkaḷ citaiyumāṟu ciṟiyavum veṇmaiyavu māya iṟāmīṉkaḷ kūṭit tākkum eṉṟa vuvamaiyāl villēntiya valiya vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ varum taṉ paṭaiyaic citaṟumāṟu piṇṭaṉatu cēṉai pōntu tākkiṉamai peṟṟām. acceyal viḷaṅka āciriyar "uṟupakai tarūum moymmūcu piṇṭaṉ" eṉṟār. eṉavē naṉkalam citaiyumāṟu iṟaviṉ kuppai tākkiṉum, akkalaṅkaḷiṉ vicaiyāl etiraḻintu avai irintōṭal pōlap piṇṭaṉ cēṉaiyu moḻinta teṉpār, "uṇmura ṇuṭaiyak kaṭanta" veṉṟār. murukakkaṭavuḷ virumpum pūvākaliṉ "kaṭavuṭ kāntaḷ" eṉṟār. tirumāl uyartta koṭiyaik kaṭavuṭ koṭi eṉpatu pōla(pari. 4.43urai)
malar, uvamaiyākupeyarāyk kaiyiṉai yuṇarttiṟṟu "koṭiyiyalār kaipōṟ kuvintaṉa mukai" eṉṟār paripāṭali lum, pari. 20.98) 'valliṉum vallārāyiṉum ceṉṟārkkuc cālaviḻ neṭuṅkuḻi niṟaiya vīcum, māal yāṉai āay eṉṟatu āay aṇṭiraṉatu koṭaimaṭamkūṟiyavāṟu cālaviḻ neṭuṅ kuḻi eṉpataṟkuc cāl eṉṉum maṭkalattu āḻnta kuḻi niṟaiya eṉiṉumām. talaiyāṟṟu nilaiiya eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇattaic cellum neṟiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇṇē nilai peṟṟa eṉiṉumām. atupōtu atu piṉmuṉṉākat tokka āṟāvataṉ, tokai, cēyavāyiṉum eṉpuḻic cemmaiyuṭaiya vāyiṉum eṉṉuraip piṉumām.
itaṉkaṇ piṇṭaṉatu cēṉai naṉṉaṉ cēṉaiyai eti rūṉṟit tākkutalum, atu nōkki naṉṉaṉ cēṉai accēṉaiyai yaḻittup pūcal māṟṟutaliṉ, itu kuṟiñciyiṉ puṟamākiya veṭci yāyiṟṟu. tuṟai pūcaṉmāṟṟu, āy aviḻ vīcutal veṭciyil uṇṭāṭṭu.
iravuk kuṟikkaṇ kūṭṭam peṟṟavaṉ peṟātāṉ pōla neñcoṭu nontu kūṟutaliṉ, meyppāṭu; taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya varuttam paṟṟiya aḻukai. payaṉ; neñcoṭu taṉ ārāmai kūṟutal.
kuṟippu: iravukkuṟikkaṇ kūṭṭam peṟṟuc  cellum talaimakaṉ taṉ neñcoṭu kūṟuvatu poruḷākap pāṭat toṭaṅkiya āciriyar mutaṟkaṇ talaiviyiṉ iyalkūṟip piṉ avaḷ tōḷnilai kūṟuvār māṭṭuṟuppākat tittaṉatu kāṉalam peruntuṟaikkaṇ kalaṅkaḷ varum ciṟappuk kūṟi ataṉaiyē uvamaiyāka iyaittup piṇṭaṉai naṉṉaṉ veṉṟamaiyum, avaṉuṭaiya pāram eṉṉum nakaram, ēḻil neṭuvarai, pāḻimalai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nalamum viḷakkip piṉṉar avaḷ kaicciṟappiṉai naḷḷiyiṉ cōlai yaṭukkattaip pukaḻu mukattāṟ kūṟip paṭip pōr maṉattait tamvayam koṇṭu ic ceytikaḷuḷ naṭunāyakamāy naṉṉaṉ pukaḻ avartam maṉatiṟ patiya vaittuḷḷamai nōkki yiṉ puṟaṟpāṟṟu. iṅṅaṉ ovvorupāṭṭaiyum viritteḻutiṉ, itu mika viriyumākaliṉ, iṉi ovvoru pāṭṭaiyum potup poruḷ kūṟumukattāl viḷakkic cellukiṉṟēṉ.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa āay pirampu eṉpatu uṇarttum pāṭṭu.
"mēṟṟuṟaik koḷīiya kaḻāliṟ kīḻttuṟai
  yukuvā raruntap pakuvā yāmai
  kampu ḷiyava ṉāka vicipiṇit
  teṇkaṭ kiṇaiyiṟ piṟaḻu mūraṉ
  iṭaineṭun teruviṟ katumeṉak kaṇṭeṉ
  poṟṟoṭi muṉkai paṟṟiṉa ṉāka
  vaṉṉā yeṉṟaṉe ṉavaṉkaiviṭ ṭaṉaṉē
  toṉṉacai cālāmai naṉṉaṉ paṟampiṟ
  ciṟukā rōṭaṉ payiṉoṭu cērttiya
  kaṟpō ṉāviṉē ṉāki maṟṟatu
  ceppaleṉ maṉṉāl yāykkē naṟṟērk
  kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉaic cōḻar marukaṉ
  neṭuṅkatir nelliṉ vallaṅ kiḻavōṉ
  nallaṭi yuḷḷā ṉākavum oṉṉār
  katava muyaṟalu muyalpa atāaṉṟu
  olipal kūntal namvayiṉ aruḷātu
  koṉṟaṉa ṉāyiṉum kolaipa ḻutaṉṟē
  aruvi yāmpal kalitta muṉṟuṟai
  naṉṉaṉ āay piram paṉṉa
  miṉṉī rōti yeṉṉainiṉ kuṟippē" (akam .356)
eṉpatu,
iḵtu irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talaimakaṟkut tōḻi talai makaḷaik kuṟainayappak kūṟiyatu. aḵtāvatu tōḻiyai matiyuṭam paṭukku mukattāl talaimakaṉ talaimakaḷ kaṇṇāṟ kāṭṭiya tōḻiyai yaṇukit taṉ kuṟaiyirantu niṟpa, avaḷ avaṟkuk kuṟainērntu talaimakaḷiṭam pōntu, "yāṉ talaimakaṉait teruviṭaik kaṇṭēṉāka, avaṉ eṉ muṉkai paṟṟa, yāṉ uṭaṉē aṉṉāy eṉṟu kūṟa, avaṉ eṉ kaiviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaṉ; yāṉum ataṉai yāykku uṇarttiṉē ṉillai; avaṉ poruṭṭu niṉkuṟippu yātu? kūṟuka." eṉa avaḷ kuṟainayantu uṭampaṭumāṟu colliyatu.
itaṉporuḷ: miṉṉalpōla oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntalai yuṭaiyāy! mēlaittuṟaikkaṭ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟaik kaḻuvutalāl, kīḻaittuṟaikkaṇ utirntu tappi, varuvaṉavaṟṟai yuṇṭaṟkut tiṟanta vāyai yuṭaiya yāmai, kampuṭkōḻi pāṭumakaṉāka viḷaṅka, vārkkaṭ ṭamainta teḷinta kaṇṇai yuṭaiya kiṇaiyeṉṉum icaikkaruvi pōlap piṟaḻum ūraṉ, neṭiya teruviṉiṭaiyil, eṉṉaik katumeṉak kaṇṭu eṉ poṉvaḷai yaṇinta muṉkaiyaip paṟṟiṉāṉāka, yāṉ añci "aṉṉāy" eṉak kūviṉēṉ; avaṉ uṭaṉē eṉkaiyai viṭṭaṉaṉ; paḻaiya viruppa mamaiyāmaiyiṉāl, naṉṉaṉatu paṟampiṉkaṇ ciṟiya paṇaiyiṉāl arakkoṭu kūṭṭappaṭṭu acaiyātu niṟkum kallaippōl acaiyāta nāviṉai yuṭaiyēṉāy, yāykkum ceppā toḻintēṉ; ākavē, nalla tēriṉaiyum virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷaiyu muṭaiya cōḻar kuṭiyō ṉākiya nīṇṭa katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya nelvayal mikka "vallam" eṉṉum ūrukkuriyōṉ piṟar taṉ nalla aṭiyai aṭaitalai niṉaiyā ṉāyiṉum, pakaivar, avaṉatu an neñcat tiṭpattaik koḷḷātu avaṉ kōpikkattakuvaṉavaṟṟai muyalutalum  ceyvar; ataṉāl avaṉ avarkaḷaiyē yaṉṟi, taḻaitta palavākiya kūntalaiyuṭaiya nam pōlum makaḷirmāṭṭu maruḷceyyātu appakaivarōṭu kolluvā ṉāyiṉum atu kuṟṟamākā taṉṟē. aruviyum āmpalum taḻaitta muṉtuṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaya naṉṉaṉatu "āay pirampu" eṉṉum nakarattai yotta niṉ karuttu yātu? kūṟuka, eṉpatu.
kal, cāṇai, arakkum kallum kūṭṭicceyta kallai vēṟu pirikkavum acaikkavum oṇṇāteṉṟu, "kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉ" eṉṟār. taṉṉai yaṭaintārkku ātaravu ceyyum iyalpiṟṟā kaliṉ, "nallaṭi" eṉṟār.
oṉṉār avaṉ veṟuppaṉavaṟṟaic ceytu vekuḷuvit talāl avaṉ avaraic cekuttal kuṟṟamaṉ ṟākaliṉ, avar makaḷiraik kōṟalum kuṟṟamaṉṟā meṉpāḷ "koṉṟaṉaṉāyiṉum kolai paḻutaṉṟē" eṉṟāḷ. ummai, koṉṟatu, kuṟṟamē yeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu. oṉṉāratu nallaṭi yuḷḷāṉākavum, avar uḷḷiyatākak karuti avaṉ veṟuppaṉavaṟṟaic ceyya muyalpa, ataṉāl avaṉ ciṉantu nampōl vāriṭattum aruḷceyyātu kōṟal kuṟṟamaṉṟu eṉṟum; nallaṭiyuḷḷiya kālattut tāṉ aruḷ ceytal vēṇṭumeṉpatai niṉaiyāṉākavum, pakaivar avaṉ aruḷ ceyyat takuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceytaliṉṟi vekuḷvaṉavaṟṟaic  ceytaliṉ avaṉ ceyyum peṇkolai paḻutākāteṉṟumām.
"cōḻar marukaṉ" eṉṟataṉāl kuṭipperumaiyaiyum "nallaṭi yuḷḷāṉ" eṉṟataṉāl avaṉatu maṟamuṭaimaiyum nōkkātu, oṉṉār takātaṉavaṟṟaic ceyya muyalvar; avaṉ avaṟṟaik kāttaṟporuṭṭu mikka ceyalaic ceytal kuṟṟamākā teṉpa tām; eṉavē, āṭavar tam kuṭipperumaiyum āṇmaiyum kāttaṟporuṭṭu makaḷirmāṭṭum aruḷ ceyyātu mikka ceyal ceyvārataliṉ, ūraṉum, tāṉ uḷḷiyatu peṟaṟ poruṭṭu maṭalēṟutal, varaipāytal mutaliya mikka ceyalkaḷaiyum ceyvaṉ; aḵtu avaṟkukkuṟṟamākā tākaliṉ, nām avaṉ vēṇṭum kuṟainērntu taṭuttal nam kaṭaṉ eṉpāḷ. "eṉṉai niṉ kuṟippu" eṉṟāḷ.
"iṭaineṭun teruviṟ katumeṉak kaṇṭu, poṟṟoṭi muṉkai paṟṟiṉaṉ" eṉṟataṉāl talaivaṉ irantupiṉṉiṉṟatum, "kaṟpōl nāviṉēṉāki maṟṟatu, ceppaleṉ maṉṉālyāykkē" eṉṟataṉāl tāṉ avaṟkuk kuṟainērntamaiyum, "eṉṉai niṉ kuṟippē" eṉṟataṉāl kuṟainayakkumāṟum, kūṟiṉāḷām. meyppāṭu: taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya icaimai poruḷākap piṟanta perumitam. payaṉ: talaimakaḷ kēṭṭuk kuṟai nayappāḷāvatu.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ taṉ pāḻinakarattiṉkaṭ poruḷ vaittu vēḷir palar kāppac ceyta ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu,
naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit
toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vaitta
poṉṉiṉum arumainaṟ kaṟintu maṉṉōṭ
ṭuṉṉala mātō veṉiṉumaḵ tollāy
taṇmaḻai tavaḻun tāḻnīr naṉantalaik
kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṭukku neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟattu
māya viruḷaḷai māykaṟ pōla
māykatil vāḻiya neñcē nāḷum
melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ nallaka nacaii
yaravirai tēru mañcuvaru ciṟuneṟi
yiravi ṉeytiyum peṟāa yaruḷvarap
pulleṉ kaṇṇai pulampukoṇ ṭulakat
tuḷḷōrk kellām perunakai yākak
kāmam kaimmika vuṟutara
vāṉā varumpaṭar talaittan tōyē," 	   (akam. 258)
eṉpatu 
iḵtu allakuṟippaṭṭup patippeyarnta talaimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.
itaṉporuḷ:- neñcē! naṉṉaṉākiya utiyaṉ kiṭṭutaṟkariya kāvalamainta pāḻi yeṉṉum nakariṉkaṇ mikavum tollō rākiya vēḷir palar kāval ceyya vaitta poṉṉiṉum ivaḷ (talaimakaḷ) peṟutaṟ kariyaḷātalait teḷiya aṟintu vaittum, avaḷai neruṅkā toḻikuvam eṉṟālum, amaiyātu, varuttukiṉṟaṉai; mēlum, nāṭōṟum meṉmaiyāṉa iyal pamainta ciṟiyavaḷuṭaiya nalla maṉaiyai virumpi, pāmpukaḷ tamakkuriya iraiyait tēṭukiṉṟa accam poruntiya iraviṟ ceṉṟum, avaḷatu kūṭṭattaip peṟṟāyillai; kaṇṭōr neñcattu irakkam eytumāṟu pulleṉṟa kaṇṇaiyuṭaiyai yāyt taṉimaiyuṟṟu varunti, ulakatti luḷḷavarkaḷellārum kaṇṭu nakaiceyyum vakaiyiṟ kāmam peruki varuttutalāl amaiyāta taṭuttaṟkariya nōyai mikat tantaṉaiyātalāl, kuḷirnta mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻap peyta maḻainīr tāḻntōṭa, ataṉiṭaiyil, peruṅkāṟ ṟeḻuntu cavaṭṭum neṭiya malaiyiṉ mikak karutta iruḷ niṟainta muḻaiñciṉuḷ viḻuntu maṟaikiṉṟa kaṟpōla nīyum viḻuntu māyvāyāka, eṉpatu.
neñcē, utiyaṉ pāḻiyiṉkaṇ vaitta poṉṉiṉum arumai naṉkaṟintu vaittum, eṉiṉum, ollāyāy, nallakam nacaii, neṟiyai iraviṉ eytiyum, peṟātoḻintaṉai; mēlum, pulleṉ kaṇṇaiyāyp pulampukoṇṭu nakaiyāka, kāmam kaimmika vuṟutara, talaittantōy ātalāl māykal pōla māyvāyāka eṉa viyaiyum.
'naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit, toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vaitta poṉṉiṉum arumai" eṉṟataṉāl talai makaḷaic ceṉṟu talaippeyaṟkuk kūṭāvakai amaintuḷḷa kāvalum, ataṉaik kaṭattaṟ karumaiyum viḷakkiya vāṟām. kūṭṭam iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭu eytap peṟāmaiyiṉ, "aṉṉōḷ tuṉṉalam" eṉṟum, piṉṉart talaimakaḷāl avviṭaiyīṭey tāmai yuṇarntaviṭattu, av voḻukkatti ṉiṭattē uḷḷam cēṟaliṉ, "aḵtollāy, nāḷum............ iraviṉ eytiyum" eṉṟum, viṉaimēṟ koṇṭavaṉ, iṭaiyi luḷavām iṭaiyīṭu kaḷāl maṉamuṭaintu pulampukoṇṭu maṭaṅkuvāṉāyiṉ, aṟivum māṉamu milaṉeṉa ulakattār ikaḻutaliṉ "ulakat tuḷḷōrk kellām perunakaiyāka" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
"aravirai tēru mañcuvaru ciṟuneṟi, iraviṉ eytiyum" eṉṟataṉāl, muṟpōntu avaḷatu tuṉṉaṟ karumaiyuṇarntatu pakaṟkuṟi yiṭaiyīṭupaṭṭa teṉpatām. eṉavē, talaimakaṉ iraviṉum pakaliṉum kuṟivaḻi yoḻuki iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭuk kūṭṭam peṟāmaiyiṉ, kalakkamuṟṟup pulampukoṇṭu neñciṉai neruṅkik kūṟuvā ṉāyiṉaṉ eṉavaṟika.
"iṉpattai veṟuttal" (tol. po. 270) eṉṟa meyp pāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattu "nalattaka nāṭiṟ kalakkamu matuvē" eṉṟatu koṇṭu, "ivvāṟu kūṟavē iccūttirattu ḷōtiya irupatu meyppāṭun talaimakaṟkum ēṟṟavakaiyāṉē koḷḷappaṭum" eṉpavākaliṉ, itaṉkaṇ  "araviraitērum añcuvaru ciṟuneṟi" yeṉṟatu ētamāytal, "pulleṉ kaṇṇai pulampukoṇṭu" eṉṟatu āṅku neñcaḻital. "ulakattu, uḷḷōrk kellām perunakaiyākak, kāmam kaimmika vuṟutara, āṉā varumpaṭar talait tantōy" eṉṟatu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkalum kalakkamumām. ēṉai meyppāṭu; aḻukaiyaic cārnta iḷivaral payaṉ neñcoṭu nērtal.
piṉvarum pāṭṭum ipporuṭṭē yākum.
arunteṟal marapiṉ kaṭavuḷ kāppap
peruntēṉ ṟūṅku nāṭukā ṇaṉantalai
yaṇaṅkuṭai varaippiṟ pāḻi yāṅkaṇ
vēṇmutu mākkaḷ viyaṉakark karanta
varuṅkala veṟukkaiyi ṉariyōḷ paṇpuniṉaintu
varuntiṉa mātō veṉiṉumaḵ tollāy
irumpaṇait toṭutta palarā ṭūca
lūrntiḻi kayiṟṟiṟ celavara varunti
neṭuneṟik kutiraik kūrvē lañci
kaṭumuṉai yalaitta koṭuvi lāṭavar
ākoḷ pūcaliṟ pāṭuciṟan teṟiyum
peruntuṭi vaḷpiṉ vīṅkupu nekiḻā
mēymaṇi yiḻanta pāmpiṉ nīnaṉi
tēmpiṉai vāḻiyeṉ ṉeñcē vēntar
koṇṭaṇi yeyiliṟ kāppuc ciṟan
tīṇṭaruṅ kuraiyaḷnam maṇaṅki yōḷē.     
ituvumatu.   			(akam. 372)
urai: eṉ neñcē! taṭuttaṟkariya teṟutaṟ ṟoḻilaic ceyyum muṟaimaiyiṉai yuṭaiya teyvam niṉṟu kāttalaic ceyya, periya tēṉaṭaikaḷ tūṅkum koṇkāṉanāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyilirukkum teyva makaḷiraiyuṭaiya malaiyaic cārnta pāḻiyeṉṉum nakariṉkaṇ vēḷirākiya mutukuṭi. makkaḷ akaṉṟa pērakatti ṉiṭattē maṟaittuvait tōmpiya ariyavākiya poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlap peṟutaṟ kariya talaimakaḷatu arumaip paṇpiṉainiṉaittu, "iṉi ivaḷaippeṟēm" eṉa veṇṇi varuttamuṟuvē māyiṉēm eṉṟu kūṟiyum. nī atu kēṭ ṭamaiyā yāyiṉai; periya mūṅkiliṟ piṇitta palarāṭum ūcaliṉatu ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum varum kayiṟṟaip pōla vaṟitē cellutal varutal eṉṟa toḻilkaṭ kañci, nīṇṭa vaḻiyiṉai yuṭaiya kutiraimalaikku uriyavaṉāṉa kūriya vēlēntiya añci yeṉpavaṉatu accam poruntiya pōrmuṉaikkaṇ niṉṟu pakaivarai varuttiya vaḷainta villēntiya vīrar pakaippulattu āviṉaik koḷḷum pōriṉkaṇ olimikku eṟiyap paṭum periya tuṭiyiṉ vāraippōla varuttattāl vimmi nekiḻntu, mēytal kāraṇamāka vumiḻnta maṇiyai yiḻantu taṭumāṟum pāmpaip pōla nī mikavum kalakka muṟṟu varuntiṉai nammai varuttiya avaḷ, vēntar pakaippulattē veṉṟu kavarntu valiyākiya aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭa matilaip pōlak kāval mikkuṟṟu neruṅkutaṟku ariyaḷāyiṉāḷ, kāṇ eṉpatu.
eṉ neñcē! pāḻiyiṉkaṭ karanta veṟukkaiyiṉ ari yōḷiṉ paṇpu niṉaintu nām varuntiṉa meṉiṉum, aḵtol lāyāy, ūcaṟkayiṟṟaip pōlac celavuvaravukaṭku añci varunti, vīṅki nekiḻntu, mēymaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōla nī tēmpiṉai; nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ, kāppuc ciṟattalāl īṇṭutaṟ kariyaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉa viyaiyum.
pakaivariṭam kavarntu koḷḷappaṭṭa eyil, cīrkēṭuṟṟa pakuti muṟṟum cīrceyyappaṭṭu ap pakaivar mīṭṭum neruṅkik kaippaṟṟāvakaik kāvalamaikkappaṭutal pōlat talaiviyoṭu nām oḻukum oḻukkam, peṟṟōrāl uṇarappaṭṭu avaḷ veḷippōtā vakai iṟceṟikkap paṭṭaḷātaliṉ iṉi nām aṇukum pāṉmai yilaḷāyiṉāḷ eṉṟum, mēlē "aruṅkala veṟukkaiyi ṉariyōḷ" eṉṟataṟ kēṟpa, "mēymaṇi yiḻanta" eṉṟum, mēymaṇi yiḻanta nākam piṉṉar uy vaḻiyiṉṟi iṟattal pōlat talaimakaḷaik kūṭutalai yiḻanta nī tēmpip piṟitupaṭuvā yeṉa iraṅkuvāṉ, "vāḻi" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. tuṭiyiṉ vaḷpu aṭikkappaṭuṅkāl vimmutalum aḵtoḻinta viṭattu nekiḻ talum pōla neñcam kūṭṭam eytiyavaḻik kaḷippāl vimmiyum, eytāvaḻik kalaṅki nekiḻntum niṟṟaliṉ, peruntuṭi vaḷpiṉ "vīṅkupu nekiḻā" eṉṟār.
kuṟiñcit tiṇaikkēṟpa ataṉ puṟaṉāya veṭci kūṟuvār, "koṭuvil āṭavar, ākoḷpūcal" kūṟiṉār.
palarāṭum ūcal irumpaṇaikkaṇ toṭukkappaṭṭa teṉṟa maiyāṉ, talaimakaṉ neñcam talaimakaḷ tōḷiṟ piṇikkap paṭṭa teṉa oru nayantōṉṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam talai makaṉaip piṇittaṟkut tōḷ iṭamātal, "piṭipayiṉṟu tarūum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōlat, tōḷkan tākak kūntaliṟ piṇittu" (akam. 276) eṉa varutal kāṇka. meyppāṭum payaṉu mavai.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ piṇṭaṉ mutaliya vēntar palarai veṉṟu avar makaḷirtam kūntalāṟ kayiṟutirittu, avartam aruṅkalam koṇarnta ceytiyaik kūṟum pāṭṭu,
"taṭantāḷ tāḻaik kuṭampai nōṉāt
  taṇṭalai kamaḻum vaṇṭupaṭu nāṟṟat
  tiruḷpurai kūntaṟ poṅkutuka ḷāṭi
  yuruḷpoli pōla vemmuṉai varunta
  vaṇittakai yallatu piṇittal tēṟṟāp
  peruntōṭ celvat tivaḷiṉu mellā
  veṟperi taḷittaṉai nīyē poṟpuṭai
  viriyuḷaip polinta pariyuṭai naṉmāṉ
  vēnta rōṭṭiya vēntuvēl naṉṉaṉ
  kūntal muraṟciyiṟ koṭitē
  maṟappaṉmā tōniṉ viṟaṟṟakai maiyē," 	       (naṟ. 270)
eṉpatu. itu tōḻi vāyil nērkiṉṟāḷ talaimakaṉai neruṅkic colli vāyil etirkoṇṭatu, uṭaṉilaik kiḷavi vakaiyāl.
urai: *ēṭā! nī pirintakkāl periya tūṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiya tāḻaip putaliṉālē kaṭṭappaṭṭa ematu ciṟiya vīṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē poṟukka muṭiyātapaṭi, cōlaiyiluḷḷa puṉṉai mutalākiya vaṟṟiṉ malarai muṭittalāl vaṇṭukaḷ moykkiṉṟa naṟunāṟṟattaiyuṭaiya iruḷotta kūntalil mikka tukaḷ paṭiya nilattilē puraṇṭu cāyntāṟpōlāka emmuṉṉēvaruntu talai yuṭaiyaḷāki aḻakaḻinta taṉmai yoṉṟallātu niṉṉai vacamākkutalait teḷiyāta periyatōḷaiyuṭaiya cel vamakaḷākiya ivaḷiṉuṅ kāṭṭil eṉṉaip peritum nī aṉpucey toḻukā niṉṟaṉai: aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum nī pirintu ceṉṟatāṉatu aḻaku poruntiya virinta piṭarimayir polivuṟṟa virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya nalla kutiraip paṭaikaḷaiyuṭaiya pakaiyaraca rākiya piṇṭaṉ mutalāṉōraip pōrilē tōṟṟōṭac ceyta ēntiya vēṟpaṭaiyaiyuṭaiya naṉṉaṉ, tāṉ appakaiyaracariṉ urimai makaḷiraip paṟṟivantu avar talaiyai maḻittu ak kūntalaik kayiṟākat tirittu, akkayiṟṟālē appakaivariṉ yāṉaiyaip piṇitta koṭumaiyiṉum, koṭiyatāy irāniṉṟatu. ātalāl niṉatu valiya takutip pāṭṭiṉai yāṉmaṟantē viṭu kiṉṟēṉ, kāṇ, eṉpatu.
talaiviyuṭaṉ etirkoṇṭamaiyāl uṭaṉilaik kiḷavi yāyiṟṟu. ivaḷeṉac cuṭṭiniṉ ṟamaiyiṉ, uṭaṉiṉṟamai teḷika.
pulavitīrkkum vāyilāka yāṉ amaiyātavaḻi eṉṉuṭa ṉum pēcātu veṟuttal ceyvāy, amaintamaiyāl eṉṉuṭaṉ pēcuvāyāyiṉai eṉṟu koṇṭu, "eṟperi taḷittaṉai" eṉṟāḷ. meyppāṭu: uvakaiyaic cārnta perumitam. payaṉ: vāyil nērtal.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ peṇkolai purinta ceyti kūṟum pāṭṭu.
"maṇṇiya ceṉṟa voṇṇuta larivai
  puṉaltaru pacuṅkāy tiṉṟataṉ ṟappaṟ
  koṉpatiṟ ṟoṉpatu kaḷiṟṟo ṭavaṇiṟai
  poṉcey pāvai koṭuppavuṅ koḷḷāṉ
  peṇkolai purinta naṉṉaṉ pōla
  varaiyā nirayattuc celīiyarō aṉṉai
  orunāḷ, nakaimuka viruntiṉaṉ vanteṉap
  pakaimuka vūriṟ ṟuñcalō vilaḷē"		 (kuṟu. 292)
eṉpatu.
itu, tōḻi iravukkuṟikkaṇ ciṟaippuṟamākak kāppu mikuti kūṟiyatu.
urai: *kuḷittaṟkuc ceṉṟa arivai, puṉalkoṇarnta piḻaikkāka avaḷ tamar eṇpattorukaḷiṟṟōṭu avaḷ niṟaiyuḷḷa poṟpāvaiyaik koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉāy, appeṇṇaik kolaipurintiṭṭa naṉṉaṉaip pōla nīkkāta nirayattup pōvāḷāka aṉṉai; orunāḷ viruntiṉaṉāka vantataṉāl pakaiyai  mukattiṟkāṭṭum tuyilutal ilaḷāyiṉāḷ ākalāṉ. e-ṟu
paṟittilaḷ; paḻamumaṉṟu; avaṉatēyeṉṉum tuṇivum illai; ivaḷ eṭātoḻiyiṉ avaṟkē kiṭaittalum aiya muṭaiyatu eṉumivai pulappaṭa "puṉaltaru pacuṅkāy" eṉap paṭṭatu. meyppāṭu: accattaic cārnta perumitam. payaṉ: iṭai yūṟuṇarttal.
iṉi, naṉṉaṉ taṉ paṭaittalaivaṉ miñili yeṉpāṉatu tantai aṉṉiyeṉpavaṉaip pōril veṉṟu avaṉ kaṇkaḷaik kaḷaintamai kāraṇamāka, kōcarkaḷaiveṉṟu avar tam nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ turattum ceyti kūṟumpāṭṭu,
"makiḻnaṉ mārpē veyyai yālnī
 aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ
 naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkkiya
 oṉṟu moḻik kōcar pōla
 vaṉkaṭ cūḻcciyum vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē"
eṉpatu.   					           (kuṟun. 73)
itu pakaṟkuṟi maṟuttu, iravukkuṟi nērntu ataṉaiyum maṟut tamaipaṭat talaimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.
urai: tōḻi! nī makiḻ naṉatu mārpaiyē virumpuvāyāyiṉai yēyaṉṟi, kuṟivaḻi yoḻukutalāl ivvoḻukkam piṟarāl uṇarappaṭiṉ varum ētattiṉai yārāyāmaiyiṉ, tāṉ avaṉai iruvakaik kuṟiyum maṟuttaliṉ, iṉik kūṭuṅkolōveṉa maṉam aḻuṅkātoḻika; naṉṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, vaṉmai poruntiya kōcar kaḷiṉ kāval maramākiya naṟiya marattiṉaittaṭintu avar tām muṉṉar iruntu pōntara nāṭṭuṭ puṟan tantupōkap poṟātavaḻi, akkōcarkku tēṭiyirunta vaṉkaṇmai mikka cūḻcciyiṟ ciṟitēṉum tām vēṇṭumākālāṉ. eṉpatu.
veyyai, vemmaiyeṉṉum uriccollaṭiyākap piṟanta kuṟippu muṟṟu. "vemmai vēṇṭal" eṉpa (tol. col. uri. 26) oṉṟumoḻi -maṉattoṭu poruntiya māṟupaṭāta col. naṉṉaṉ, pōkkiya, kōcar eṉa viyaiyum. naṉṉaṉāṟṟurattap paṭṭa kōcar eṉpatām. kōcar oṉṟu moḻiyuṭaiyareṉpatu, "oṉṟu moḻik kōcarkkoṉṟu muraṇ pōkiya, kaṭuntērt titiyaṉ (akam. 196) eṉpataṉālumuṇarka. "iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭaikaṉmār, ikaliṉareṟinta vakalilai murukkiṉ, perumarak kampam pōlap, porunarkkulaiyā niṉvalaṉ" (puṟam. 169) eṉpataṉāl ivar paṭaip payiṟci mikutiyu muṭaiyar eṉpatu peṟutum.
ikkōcar tāmum tam naṉmoḻiyālum, paṭaip payiṟci yālum tammai viyantu, vaṉkaṇmaiyum cūḻcciyum ilarā yoḻiya, naṉṉaṉ avai yiraṇṭu muṭaiyaṉāy ivaraiveṉṟā ṉeṉavē, nīyummakiḻnaṉ mārpē virumpi, avaṉ pirivāṟṟātu kalaṅki, ivvoḻukkattā ṉuḷatākum ētattai niṉaiyā tiruntaṉaiyeṉṟum, akkōcar tāmum vaṉkaṭcūṭciyuṭaiya rāyiṉ naṉṉaṉ veṉṟiyeytutalākātavāṟupōla, nāmum vaṉkaṇmaiyum cūḻcciyum koḷḷiṉ, oḻukkattāluḷavākum ētaṅkaḷai vārāmaṟ kākkalām eṉṟum, ivvāṟu namnalaṅ karutiyē ceyya vēṇṭutaliṉ, nī aḻuṅkutal kūṭātu eṉpāḷ, "aḻiyal" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
